  			this.zoomInEffect.enabled = options.zoomInEffect;
  		}
  		if (options.zoomInFactor !== undefined) {
  			this.zoomInEffect.factor = options.zoomInFactor;
  		}
  		if (options.zoomInSpringConfig !== undefined) {
  			this.zoomInEffect.stiffness =
  				options.zoomInSpringConfig.stiffness || SCALE_SPRING_PRESETS.zoomIn;
  		}
  
  		// Create domain accessor object
  		this.domain = this.#createDomainAccessor();
  	}
  
  	// ===========================================================================
  	// DOMAIN ACCESSOR
  	// ===========================================================================
  
  	#createDomainAccessor() {
  		const self = this;
  		return {
  			/** Current render domain (for reactive access) */
  			get current() {
  				self._version; // Register Svelte dependency
  				self.#domainCurrentBuffer[0] = self.#renderMin;
  				self.#domainCurrentBuffer[1] = self.#renderMax;
  				return self.#domainCurrentBuffer;
  			},
  
  			/** Target domain (what we're animating toward) */
  			get target() {
  				return [self.#springPair.targetA, self.#springPair.targetB];
  			},
  
  			/** Set domain with optional instant mode */
  			set(newValue, options = {}) {
  				if (options.instant) {
  					self.#setInstant(newValue[0], newValue[1]);
  				} else {
  					self.#setTarget(newValue[0], newValue[1]);
  				}
  			}
  		};
  	}
  
  	// ===========================================================================
  	// PHYSICS INTERFACE (called by GPUCoordinator/AnimationLoop)
  	// ===========================================================================
  
  	/**
  	 * Process accumulated input before physics step.
  	 * Called by GPUCoordinator at the start of each frame.
  	 */
  	_applyInput() {
  		// Process accumulated zoom
  		if (this.#zoomAccumulator.hasUpdates) {
  			const scaleFactor = Math.exp(this.#zoomAccumulator.logScale);
  			this.#applyZoomToTarget(this.#zoomAccumulator.center, scaleFactor);
  
  			// Reset accumulator
  			this.#zoomAccumulator.logScale = 0;
  			this.#zoomAccumulator.hasUpdates = false;
  		}
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Advance physics by one fixed timestep.
  	 * Called by AnimationLoop at fixed rate (360Hz).
  	 */
  	step() {
  		this.#springPair.step();
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Update render state with extrapolation.
  	 * Called once per frame by AnimationLoop.
  	 *
  	 * @param {number} extrapolationDt - Time to extrapolate in seconds
  	 */
  	updateRenderState(extrapolationDt) {
  		// const rendered = this.#springPair.getRenderable(extrapolationDt);
  		this.#springPair.getRenderableInto(extrapolationDt, this.#springOutput);
  		this.#renderMin = this.#springOutput[0];
  		this.#renderMax = this.#springOutput[1];
  		// this.#renderMin = rendered.a;
  		// this.#renderMax = rendered.b;
  		this.#updateGpuState();
  
  		// Trigger Svelte reactivity
  		// this._version++;
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Check if spring is at rest.
  	 * Used by AnimationLoop to determine when to stop.
  	 */
  	isAtRest() {
  		return this.#springPair.isAtRest();
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Called by GPUCoordinator when this scale stops animating.
  	 */
  	_markAtRest() {
  		this.#isAnimating = false;
  	}
  
  	// ===========================================================================
  	// INPUT METHODS
  	// ===========================================================================
  
  	/**
  	 * Start a pan interaction.
  	 * @param {object} context - Pan context with initialDomain, screenToDataRatio, etc.
  	 */
  	setPanContext(context) {
  		// console.log(`=== setPanContext for ${this.name} (${this.id}) ===`);
  		// console.log('axis:', context.axis);
  		// console.log('#isAnimating before:', this.#isAnimating);
  		// console.log('#gpuCoordinator exists:', !!this.#gpuCoordinator);
  		this.#panContext = {
  			initialMin: this.#springPair.currentA,
  			initialMax: this.#springPair.currentB,
  			domainWidth: this.#springPair.currentB - this.#springPair.currentA,
  			screenToDataRatio: context.screenToDataRatio,
  			axis: context.axis,
  			startScreenPos: context.initialScreenPos
  		};
  
  		// Grab springs - snaps target to current, zeros velocity
  		// this.#springPair.grab();
  		this.#setStiffness(SCALE_SPRING_PRESETS.pan);
  		this.#ensureAnimating();
  
  		// console.log('#isAnimating after:', this.#isAnimating);
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Update pan position.
  	 * @param {number} screenX - Current screen X position
  	 * @param {number} screenY - Current screen Y position
  	 */
  	updatePanPosition(screenX, screenY) {
  		if (!this.#panContext) return;
  
  		const ctx = this.#panContext;
  		let dataDelta = 0;
  
  		if (ctx.axis === 'x') {
  			const screenDelta = screenX - ctx.startScreenPos;
  			dataDelta = -(screenDelta * ctx.screenToDataRatio);
  		} else {
  			const screenDelta = screenY - ctx.startScreenPos;
  			dataDelta = screenDelta * ctx.screenToDataRatio;
  		}
  
  		let newMin = ctx.initialMin + dataDelta;
  		let newMax = newMin + ctx.domainWidth;
  
  		// Clamp to domain limits
  		const clamped = this.#clampDomain(newMin, newMax);
  		this.#springPair.setTarget(clamped.min, clamped.max);
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * End pan interaction.
  	 */
  	endPan() {
  		this.#panContext = null;
  		this.#setStiffness(SCALE_SPRING_PRESETS.default);
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Accumulate zoom input (for coalesced wheel events).
  	 * @param {number} dataCenter - Zoom center in data coordinates
  	 * @param {number} scaleFactor - Zoom scale factor (>1 = zoom in)
  	 */
  	accumulateZoom(dataCenter, scaleFactor) {
  		this.#zoomAccumulator.logScale += Math.log(scaleFactor);
  		this.#zoomAccumulator.center = dataCenter;
  		this.#zoomAccumulator.hasUpdates = true;
  		this.#setStiffness(SCALE_SPRING_PRESETS.zoom);
  		this.#ensureAnimating();
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Reset zoom to show full extent.
  	 */
  	resetZoom() {
  		const [min, max] = this.effectiveExtent;
  		if (!isFinite(min) || !isFinite(max) || min === max) return;
  
  		if (!this.ready && this.zoomInEffect.enabled) {
  			// First time - do zoom-in effect from expanded view
  			this.#setStiffness(this.zoomInEffect.stiffness);
  
  			const center = (min + max) / 2;
  			const range = max - min;
  			const factor = this.zoomInEffect.factor;
  			const expandedMin = center - (range * factor) / 2;
  			const expandedMax = center + (range * factor) / 2;
  
  			// Set current position to expanded, target to actual
  			this.#springPair.set(expandedMin, expandedMax);
  			this.#springPair.setTarget(min, max);
  			this.#ensureAnimating();
  		} else {
  			// Normal reset
  			this.#setStiffness(SCALE_SPRING_PRESETS.reset);
  			this.#springPair.setTarget(min, max);
  			this.#ensureAnimating();
  		}
  	}
  
  	// ===========================================================================
  	// D3 SCALE INTERFACE
  	// ===========================================================================
  
  	/**
  	 * Get a D3 scale configured for the given axis and size.
  	 * Reuses internal scale object to avoid allocation.
  	 */
  	getBaseScale(axis, size) {
  		if (axis === 'x') {
  			this.#sharedScale.range([0, size]);
  		} else {
  			this.#sharedScale.range([size, 0]);
  		}
  		return this.#sharedScale;
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Update a D3 scale's domain with current render values.
  	 * Registers Svelte dependency via _version.
  	 */
  	updateScaleDomain(scale) {
  		this._version; // Register dependency
  		scale.domain([this.#renderMin, this.#renderMax]);
  		return scale;
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Reactive getter for domain (triggers Svelte updates).
  	 */
  	get domainForReactivity() {
  		this._version;
  		return [this.#renderMin, this.#renderMax];
  	}
  
  	// ===========================================================================
  	// COORDINATE TRANSFORMS
  	// ===========================================================================
  
  	/**
  	 * Convert data value to screen position.
  	 */
  	scaleDataToScreen(dataVal, rangeSize, isYAxis = false) {
  		const min = this.#renderMin;
  		const max = this.#renderMax;
  		const domainSpan = max - min;
  
  		if (domainSpan === 0) return 0;
  
  		const t = (dataVal - min) / domainSpan;
  
  		if (isYAxis) {
  			return rangeSize - t * rangeSize;
  		} else {
  			return t * rangeSize;
  		}
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Convert screen position to data value.
  	 */
  	invertScreenToData(screenVal, rangeSize, isYAxis = false) {
  		const min = this.#renderMin;
  		const max = this.#renderMax;
  
  		if (rangeSize === 0) return min;
  
  		const t = screenVal / rangeSize;
  
  		if (isYAxis) {
  			return max - t * (max - min);
  		} else {
  			return min + t * (max - min);
  		}
  	}
  
  	// ===========================================================================
  	// EXTENT MANAGEMENT
  	// ===========================================================================
  
  	/**
  	 * Register a mark's data extent with this scale.
  	 */
  	addMark(range, identifier) {
  		let processedRange;
  
  		if (range.length === 2) {
  			processedRange = [Math.min(range[0], range[1]), Math.max(range[0], range[1])];
  		} else if (range.length > 2) {
  			processedRange = d3Extent(range);
  		} else if (range.length === 1) {
  			processedRange = [range[0], range[0]];
  		} else {
  			return this;
  		}
  
  		this.#extents.set(identifier, processedRange);
  
  		if (this.#enableUpdates) {
  			this.updateExtentFromExtents();
  		}
  
  		return this;
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Remove a mark's extent from this scale.
  	 */
  	removeMark(identifier) {
  		if (this.#extents.has(identifier)) {
  			this.#extents.delete(identifier);
  
  			if (this.#enableUpdates) {
  				this.updateExtentFromExtents();
  			}
  		}
  		return this;
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Recompute extent from all registered marks.
  	 */
  	updateExtentFromExtents(nice = true) {
  		if (!this.#enableUpdates || this.#extents.size === 0) return;
  
  		const extents = Array.from(this.#extents.values());
  		const minStart = Math.min(...extents.map((r) => r[0]));
  		const maxEnd = Math.max(...extents.map((r) => r[1]));
  
  		if (nice) {
  			const niceDomain = scaleLinear().domain([minStart, maxEnd]).nice(5).domain();
  			this.#extent[0] = niceDomain[0];
  			this.#extent[1] = niceDomain[1];
  		} else {
  			this.#extent[0] = minStart;
  			this.#extent[1] = maxEnd;
  		}
  
  		this.resetDomainLimits();
  		this.resetZoom();
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Get the effective extent (respects frozen state).
  	 */
  	get effectiveExtent() {
  		return this.#enableUpdates
  			? [this.#extent[0], this.#extent[1]]
  			: this.#frozenExtent || [this.#extent[0], this.#extent[1]];
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Reset domain limits based on current extent and zoom limits.
  	 */
  	resetDomainLimits() {
  		const [min, max] = this.effectiveExtent;
  		const w = max - min;
  		this.domainLimits.min = min - this.zoomLimits.max * w;
  		this.domainLimits.max = max + this.zoomLimits.max * w;
  		this.domainLimits.minWidth = w / this.zoomLimits.min;
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Mark scale as ready (after initial data load).
  	 */
  	setReady() {
  		this.ready = true;
  	}
  
  	// ===========================================================================
  	// PRIVATE METHODS
  	// ===========================================================================
  
  	#setTarget(min, max) {
  		this.#springPair.setTarget(min, max);
  		this.#ensureAnimating();
  	}
  
  	#setInstant(min, max) {
  		this.#springPair.set(min, max);
  		this.#renderMin = min;
  		this.#renderMax = max;
  		this.#updateGpuState();
  		this._version++;
  	}
  
  	#setStiffness(stiffness) {
  		this.#springPair.configure(stiffness);
  	}
  
  	#updateGpuState() {
  		const min = this.#renderMin;
  		const max = this.#renderMax;
  		const range = max - min;
  
  		this.gpuState.domainMin = min;
  		this.gpuState.domainMax = max;
  		this.gpuState.domainScale = range === 0 ? 0 : 1 / range;
  	}
  
  	#ensureAnimating() {
  		if (!this.#isAnimating && this.#gpuCoordinator) {
  			this.#isAnimating = true;
  			this.#gpuCoordinator.addAnimatingScale(this);
  		}
  	}
  
  	#applyZoomToTarget(center, scaleFactor) {
  		const min = this.#springPair.targetA;
  		const max = this.#springPair.targetB;
  		const curWidth = max - min;
  		const newWidth = curWidth / scaleFactor;
  
  		// Clamp to minimum width
  		const safeWidth = Math.max(newWidth, this.domainLimits.minWidth);
  
  		// Maintain zoom center position
  		const ratio = (center - min) / curWidth;
  		let newMin = center - ratio * safeWidth;
  		let newMax = newMin + safeWidth;
  
  		// Clamp to domain limits
  		const clamped = this.#clampDomain(newMin, newMax);
  		this.#springPair.setTarget(clamped.min, clamped.max);
  	}
  
  	#clampDomain(min, max) {
  		const limits = this.domainLimits;
  		const width = max - min;
  		const limitWidth = limits.max - limits.min;
  
  		if (width > limitWidth) {
  			this.#clampResult.min = limits.min;
  			this.#clampResult.max = limits.max;
  			return this.#clampResult;
  		}
  
  		if (min < limits.min) {
  			min = limits.min;
  			max = min + width;
  		}
  		if (max > limits.max) {
  			max = limits.max;
  			min = max - width;
  			if (min < limits.min) {
  				min = limits.min;
  			}
  		}
  
  		this.#clampResult.min = min;
  		this.#clampResult.max = max;
  		return this.#clampResult;
  	}
  
  	// ===========================================================================
  	// CLEANUP
  	// ===========================================================================
  
  	destroy() {
  		this.#isAnimating = false;
  		if (this.#gpuCoordinator?.removeAnimatingScale) {
  			this.#gpuCoordinator.removeAnimatingScale(this);
  		}
  	}
  }
</src/lib/scales/LinearScaleState.svelte.js>

<src/lib/scales/BandScaleState.svelte.js>
  // src/lib/scales/BandScaleState.svelte.js
  
  import { scaleBand } from 'd3-scale';
  
  /**
   * BandScaleState manages categorical (band/point) scales.
   *
   * Key responsibilities:
   * - Domain management (unique categories from multiple marks)
   * - Category-to-index mapping for GPU rendering
   * - D3 scale creation for axes and SVG components
   * - Configuration for GPU band scale calculations
   *
   * Features:
   * - Freeze/debounce support for performance
   * - Extent tracking from multiple marks
   * - GPU-ready index mapping
   * - Full D3 compatibility
   */
  export class BandScaleState {
  	// =========================================================================
  	// IDENTITY & TYPE
  	// =========================================================================
  	id = crypto.randomUUID();
  	name = $state('');
  	type = 'band';
  	ready = $state(false);
  	_version = $state(0);
  
  	// =========================================================================
  	// DOMAIN STATE
  	// =========================================================================
  	#domain = $state([]);
  	#extents = new Map(); // Stores domains from different marks
  
  	// =========================================================================
  	// CONFIGURATION (D3 band scale parameters)
  	// =========================================================================
  	#padding = 0.1;
  	#paddingInner = null;
  	#paddingOuter = null;
  	#align = 0.5;
  
  	// =========================================================================
  	// FREEZE/DEBOUNCE FUNCTIONALITY
  	// =========================================================================
  	#enableUpdates = $state(true);
  	#frozenDomain = null;
  
  	// =========================================================================
  	// D3 SCALE INSTANCE (Reused for performance)
  	// =========================================================================
  	#sharedScale = scaleBand();
  
  	// =========================================================================
  	// DERIVED STATE
  	// =========================================================================
  
  	/**
  	 * Category-to-index mapping (derived)
  	 * Used by marks to convert category strings to GPU indices
  	 */
  	#categoryIndexMap = $derived.by(() => {
  		const domain = this.#enableUpdates ? this.#domain : this.#frozenDomain || this.#domain;
  
  		const map = new Map();
  		domain.forEach((category, index) => {
  			map.set(category, index);
  		});
  		return map;
  	});
  
  	/**
  	 * GPU state (derived) - for compatibility with coordinator
  	 */
  	gpuState = $derived.by(() => {
  		const categoryCount = this.#categoryIndexMap.size;
  
  		return {
  			domainMin: 0,
  			domainMax: Math.max(0, categoryCount - 1),
  			categoryCount: categoryCount,
  			domainScale: 0
  		};
  	});
  
  	// =========================================================================
  	// CONSTRUCTOR
  	// =========================================================================
  
  	constructor(name, domain = [], config = {}) {
  		this.name = name;
  		this.#domain = [...domain];
  
  		// Apply configuration with defaults
  		this.#padding = config.padding ?? 0.1;
  		this.#paddingInner = config.paddingInner ?? null;
  		this.#paddingOuter = config.paddingOuter ?? null;
  		this.#align = config.align ?? 0.5;
  
  		console.log(`🏗️ BandScaleState "${name}" created:`, {
  			initialDomain: domain.length,
  			padding: this.#padding,
  			paddingInner: this.#paddingInner,
  			paddingOuter: this.#paddingOuter,
  			align: this.#align
  		});
  	}
  
  	// =========================================================================
  	// LIFECYCLE
  	// =========================================================================
  
  	setReady() {
  		this.ready = true;
  		console.log(`✅ BandScaleState "${this.name}" marked as ready`);
  	}
  
  	destroy() {
  		this.#frozenDomain = null;
  		this.#extents.clear();
  	}
  
  	// =========================================================================
  	// FREEZE/DEBOUNCE CONTROL
  	// =========================================================================
  
  	/**
  	 * Enable or disable scale updates
  	 * When disabled, domain is frozen at current state
  	 */
  	setUpdatesEnabled(enabled) {
  		const wasEnabled = this.#enableUpdates;
  		this.#enableUpdates = enabled;
  
  		if (wasEnabled && !enabled) {
  			// Freezing - capture current domain
  			this.#frozenDomain = [...this.#domain];
  			console.log(`❄️ BandScale "${this.name}" frozen at ${this.#frozenDomain.length} categories`);
  		} else if (!wasEnabled && enabled) {
  			// Unfreezing - restore updates
  			this.#frozenDomain = null;
  			this.updateDomainFromExtents();
  			console.log(`🔥 BandScale "${this.name}" unfrozen`);
  		}
  	}
  
  	get updatesEnabled() {
  		return this.#enableUpdates;
  	}
  
  	get effectiveDomain() {
  		return this.#enableUpdates ? this.#domain : this.#frozenDomain || this.#domain;
  	}
  
  	// =========================================================================
  	// GPU READINESS
  	// =========================================================================
  
  	/**
  	 * Check if the scale is ready for GPU processing
  	 */
  	get isReadyForGPU() {
  		return (
  			this.ready &&
  			this.effectiveDomain.length > 0 &&
  			this.#categoryIndexMap instanceof Map &&
  			this.#extents.size > 0
  		);
  	}
  
  	// =========================================================================
  	// DOMAIN MANAGEMENT
  	// =========================================================================
  
  	/**
  	 * Add categorical data from a mark
  	 * @param {Array} categories - Array of category values
  	 * @param {string} identifier - Mark identifier
  	 */
  	addMark(categories, identifier) {
  		const uniqueCategories = [...new Set(categories.filter((c) => c != null))];
  
  		this.#extents.set(identifier, uniqueCategories);
  
  		if (this.#enableUpdates) {
  			this.updateDomainFromExtents();
  		}
  
  		return this;
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Remove a mark's categories from domain calculation
  	 * @param {string} identifier - Mark identifier
  	 */
  	removeMark(identifier) {
  		if (this.#extents.has(identifier)) {
  			this.#extents.delete(identifier);
  
  			if (this.#enableUpdates) {
  				this.updateDomainFromExtents();
  			}
  		}
  		return this;
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Update domain based on all categories from all marks
  	 * Preserves order of first appearance
  	 */
  	updateDomainFromExtents() {
  		if (!this.#enableUpdates) {
  			return;
  		}
  		if (this.#extents.size === 0) {
  			this.#domain = [];
  			this._version++;
  			return;
  		}
  		const allCategories = [];
  		const seen = new Set();
  		for (const categories of this.#extents.values()) {
  			for (const category of categories) {
  				if (!seen.has(category)) {
  					seen.add(category);
  					allCategories.push(category);
  				}
  			}
  		}
  		this.#domain = allCategories;
  		this._version++;
  	}
  
  	// =========================================================================
  	// GPU DATA INTERFACE
  	// =========================================================================
  
  	/**
  	 * Convert categorical data to indices for GPU processing
  	 *
  	 * @param {Array} categoryData - Array of category values from mark data
  	 * @param {string} markId - Identifier for the mark (for debugging)
  	 * @returns {Float32Array} Array of indices corresponding to categories
  	 */
  	processDataForGPU(categoryData, markId = 'unknown') {
  		const categoryMap = this.#categoryIndexMap;
  
  		// Safety check - ensure map is valid
  		if (!(categoryMap instanceof Map)) {
  			console.error(`🚨 BandScale "${this.name}": categoryMap is not a Map:`, categoryMap);
  			const fallbackMap = new Map();
  			this.effectiveDomain.forEach((category, index) => {
  				fallbackMap.set(category, index);
  			});
  			return this.#processWithMap(categoryData, fallbackMap, markId);
  		}
  
  		return this.#processWithMap(categoryData, categoryMap, markId);
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Helper method to process data with a given category map
  	 * @private
  	 */
  	#processWithMap(categoryData, categoryMap, markId) {
  		const unmappedCategories = new Set();
  
  		const indices = new Float32Array(
  			categoryData.map((category) => {
  				const index = categoryMap.get(category);
  				if (index !== undefined) {
  					return index;
  				} else {
  					unmappedCategories.add(category);
  					return 0; // Default to first category index
  				}
  			})
  		);
  
  		// Warn about unmapped categories in development
  		if (unmappedCategories.size > 0) {
  			console.warn(
  				`🔶 BandScale "${this.name}": Unmapped categories for mark "${markId}":`,
  				Array.from(unmappedCategories).slice(0, 5),
  				unmappedCategories.size > 5 ? `... and ${unmappedCategories.size - 5} more` : '',
  				`\nValid categories:`,
  				this.effectiveDomain
  			);
  		}
  
  		return indices;
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Get configuration for GPU coordinator to write to SSBO
  	 * This is called by GPUCoordinator.fillAllScales()
  	 *
  	 * @returns {Object} Configuration for GPU band scale calculations
  	 */
  	getConfig() {
  		// Resolve effective padding values
  		const paddingInner = this.#paddingInner !== null ? this.#paddingInner : this.#padding;
  
  		const paddingOuter = this.#paddingOuter !== null ? this.#paddingOuter : this.#padding;
  
  		return {
  			categoryCount: this.effectiveDomain.length,
  			paddingInner: paddingInner,
  			paddingOuter: paddingOuter,
  			align: this.#align
  		};
  	}
  
  	// =========================================================================
  	// D3-COMPATIBLE API (for axes, legends, etc.)
  	// =========================================================================
  
  	/**
  	 * Get D3 band scale configured for rendering
  	 * @param {string} axis - 'x' or 'y'
  	 * @param {number} innerWidthOrHeight - Chart inner dimension
  	 * @returns {Function} D3 band scale
  	 */
  	getBaseScale(axis, innerWidthOrHeight) {
  		this.#sharedScale.range(axis === 'x' ? [0, innerWidthOrHeight] : [innerWidthOrHeight, 0]);
  
  		if (this.#paddingInner !== null && this.#paddingOuter !== null) {
  			this.#sharedScale.paddingInner(this.#paddingInner).paddingOuter(this.#paddingOuter);
  		} else {
  			this.#sharedScale.padding(this.#padding);
  		}
  
  		if (this.#align !== 0.5) {
  			this.#sharedScale.align(this.#align);
  		}
  
  		return this.#sharedScale;
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Update scale domain (for D3 scale)
  	 * @param {Function} scale - D3 scale to update
  	 */
  	updateScaleDomain(scale) {
  		this._version; // Register svelte dependency
  		scale.domain(this.effectiveDomain);
  		return scale; // Return the function itself
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Get the current domain (array of categories)
  	 */
  	domain() {
  		return [...this.effectiveDomain];
  	}
  
  	get domainForReactivity() {
  		this._version;
  		return this.effectiveDomain;
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Calculate bandwidth for a given range size (CPU-side)
  	 * Useful for axes, tooltips, etc.
  	 */
  	bandwidth(rangeSize) {
  		const config = this.getConfig();
  		const n = config.categoryCount;
  
  		if (n === 0) return 0;
  
  		const step = rangeSize / Math.max(1, n - config.paddingInner + config.paddingOuter * 2);
  		return step * (1 - config.paddingInner);
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Calculate step size for a given range size (CPU-side)
  	 */
  	step(rangeSize) {
  		const config = this.getConfig();
  		const n = config.categoryCount;
  
  		if (n === 0) return 0;
  
  		return rangeSize / Math.max(1, n - config.paddingInner + config.paddingOuter * 2);
  	}
  
  	// =========================================================================
  	// LEGACY/COMPATIBILITY METHODS
  	// =========================================================================
  
  	/**
  	 * Get positioning information for a category (for compatibility)
  	 */
  	getPosition(category) {
  		const index = this.effectiveDomain.indexOf(category);
  		if (index === -1) return { current: 0 };
  
  		return {
  			current: index / Math.max(1, this.effectiveDomain.length - 1)
  		};
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Get category index map (for debugging)
  	 */
  	getCategoryIndexMap() {
  		return new Map(this.#categoryIndexMap);
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Get all mark extents (for debugging)
  	 */
  	getExtents() {
  		return Object.fromEntries(this.#extents);
  	}
  
  	// No-op methods for API consistency with LinearScaleState
  	applyZoomDomain() {
  		return;
  	}
  	applyPanDomain() {
  		return;
  	}
  	resetZoom() {
  		return;
  	}
  
  	// =========================================================================
  	// DEBUGGING
  	// =========================================================================
  
  	/**
  	 * Get comprehensive debug information
  	 */
  	getDebugInfo() {
  		return {
  			name: this.name,
  			id: this.id,
  			type: this.type,
  			ready: this.ready,
  			isReadyForGPU: this.isReadyForGPU,
  
  			// Domain state
  			updatesEnabled: this.#enableUpdates,
  			currentDomain: [...this.#domain],
  			frozenDomain: this.#frozenDomain ? [...this.#frozenDomain] : null,
  			effectiveDomain: [...this.effectiveDomain],
  			categoryCount: this.effectiveDomain.length,
  
  			// Extent tracking
  			extentsCount: this.#extents.size,
  			extents: Object.fromEntries(this.#extents),
  
  			// Configuration
  			configuration: {
  				padding: this.#padding,
  				paddingInner: this.#paddingInner,
  				paddingOuter: this.#paddingOuter,
  				align: this.#align
  			},
  
  			// GPU config
  			gpuConfig: this.getConfig(),
  
  			// Category mapping
  			categoryIndexMap: Object.fromEntries(this.#categoryIndexMap),
  
  			// GPU state
  			gpuState: this.gpuState
  		};
  	}
  }
</src/lib/scales/BandScaleState.svelte.js>

<src/lib/scales/ColorScaleState.svelte.js>
  // src/lib/scales/ColorScaleState.svelte.js
  import {
  	createD3Scale,
  	bakeGradient,
  	bakePalette,
  	getSchemeType,
  	parseColorToRGBA
  } from './color-utils.js';
  
  /**
   * Reactive state for a GPU-backed color scale.
   * 
   * Handles both continuous (sequential, diverging, cyclical) and discrete (ordinal) scales.
   * Manages GPU slot allocation and buffer uploads.
   */
  export class ColorScaleState {
  	// Identity
  	#field;
  	#registry;
  	#gpuCoordinator;
  
  	// GPU slot indices (allocated from registry)
  	#configSlot = -1;
  	#rampSlot = -1;      // Used by continuous scales
  	#paletteSlot = -1;   // Used by ordinal scales
  
  	// Scale configuration
  	#scheme;
  	#scaleType; // 'ordinal' | 'sequential' | 'diverging' | 'cyclical'
  	#reverse;
  	#midpoint; // For diverging scales
  
  	// Reactive state
  	domain = $state([]);
  	ready = $state(false);
  
  	// D3 scale instance (for legends and CPU lookups)
  	#d3Scale = null;
  
  	// Ordinal category mapping (string -> 1-based index)
  	#categoryMap = new Map();
  
  	/**
  	 * @param {Object} config - Scale configuration
  	 * @param {string} config.field - Field name this scale maps
  	 * @param {string} config.scheme - D3 color scheme name (e.g., 'viridis', 'tableau10')
  	 * @param {Array} config.domain - Domain values
  	 * @param {boolean} [config.reverse=false] - Reverse the color direction
  	 * @param {number} [config.midpoint] - Explicit midpoint for diverging scales
  	 * @param {Object} registry - ScaleRegistry instance (for slot allocation)
  	 * @param {Object} gpuCoordinator - GPUCoordinator instance (for buffer writes)
  	 */
  	constructor(config, registry, gpuCoordinator) {
  		this.#field = config.field;
  		this.#scheme = config.scheme || 'viridis';
  		this.#reverse = config.reverse ?? false;
  		this.#midpoint = config.midpoint;
  		this.#registry = registry;
  		this.#gpuCoordinator = gpuCoordinator;
  
  		// Determine scale type from scheme
  		this.#scaleType = getSchemeType(this.#scheme) || 'sequential';
  
  		// Set initial domain
  		this.domain = config.domain || [];
  
  		// Allocate GPU slots
  		this.#allocateSlots();
  
  		// Initial GPU upload (if domain is ready)
  		if (this.domain.length > 0) {
  			this.#uploadToGPU();
  		}
  	}
  
  	// ============================================================
  	// GETTERS
  	// ============================================================
  
  	get field() {
  		return this.#field;
  	}
  
  	get scheme() {
  		return this.#scheme;
  	}
  
  	get scaleType() {
  		return this.#scaleType;
  	}
  
  	get isOrdinal() {
  		return this.#scaleType === 'ordinal';
  	}
  
  	get isContinuous() {
  		return this.#scaleType !== 'ordinal';
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * GPU slot information for registry cleanup.
  	 */
  	get gpuSlots() {
  		return {
  			configSlot: this.#configSlot,
  			rampSlot: this.#rampSlot,
  			paletteSlot: this.#paletteSlot
  		};
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * The config slot index (used by marks for colorScaleIndex).
  	 */
  	get gpuIndex() {
  		return this.#configSlot;
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * D3 scale for legends and CPU-side color lookups.
  	 */
  	get d3Scale() {
  		return this.#d3Scale;
  	}
  
  	// ============================================================
  	// SLOT ALLOCATION
  	// ============================================================
  
  	#allocateSlots() {
  		// Always need a config slot
  		this.#configSlot = this.#registry.allocateColorSlot();
  
  		// Allocate type-specific slot
  		if (this.isOrdinal) {
  			this.#paletteSlot = this.#registry.allocatePaletteSlot();
  		} else {
  			this.#rampSlot = this.#registry.allocateRampSlot();
  		}
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Swap slots when changing between ordinal and continuous.
  	 */
  	#swapSlots(newTypeIsOrdinal) {
  		if (newTypeIsOrdinal) {
  			this.#registry.releaseRampSlot(this.#rampSlot);
  			this.#rampSlot = -1;
  			this.#paletteSlot = this.#registry.allocatePaletteSlot();
  		} else {
  			this.#registry.releasePaletteSlot(this.#paletteSlot);
  			this.#paletteSlot = -1;
  			this.#rampSlot = this.#registry.allocateRampSlot();
  		}
  	}
  
  	// ============================================================
  	// DATA PROCESSING (Required by Marks)
  	// ============================================================
  
  	/**
  	 * Converts raw data into GPU-ready values.
  	 * - Ordinal: string -> u32 index (1-based, 0 = null/unknown)
  	 * - Continuous: number -> f32 value (pass-through)
  	 * 
  	 * @param {Array} data - Raw data values
  	 * @returns {Float32Array} GPU-ready values
  	 */
  	processDataForGPU(data) {
  		if (this.isOrdinal) {
  			const indices = new Float32Array(data.length);
  			for (let i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
  				const val = data[i];
  				// 0 = null/fallback, 1..N = valid categories
  				indices[i] = (val == null) ? 0 : (this.#categoryMap.get(val) ?? 0);
  			}
  			return indices;
  		}
  
  		// Continuous: pass through, just ensure Float32Array
  		if (data instanceof Float32Array) return data;
  		return new Float32Array(data);
  	}
  
  	// ============================================================
  	// GPU UPLOAD
  	// ============================================================
  
  	#uploadToGPU() {
  		if (!this.#gpuCoordinator) {
  			console.warn('[ColorScaleState] No GPU coordinator, skipping upload');
  			return;
  		}
  
  		if (this.domain.length === 0) {
  			return;
  		}
  
  		// Create D3 scale with current domain (for CPU/legend use)
  		this.#d3Scale = createD3Scale(this.#scheme, this.domain, {
  			reverse: this.#reverse,
  			midpoint: this.#midpoint
  		});
  
  		if (this.isOrdinal) {
  			this.#uploadOrdinal();
  		} else {
  			this.#uploadContinuous();
  		}
  
  		this.ready = true;
  	}
  
  	#uploadContinuous() {
  		// Bake gradient from configured D3 scale
  		const rampData = bakeGradient(this.#d3Scale, 256);
  		this.#gpuCoordinator.writeColorRamp(this.#rampSlot, rampData);
  
  		// Build and upload config struct
  		const config = this.#buildColorConfig({
  			type: this.#getScaleTypeInt(),
  			rampOffset: this.#rampSlot * 256,
  			domainMin: this.domain[0],
  			domainMax: this.domain[this.domain.length - 1]
  		});
  		this.#gpuCoordinator.writeColorConfig(this.#configSlot, config);
  	}
  
  	#uploadOrdinal() {
  		// Rebuild category map (string -> 1-based index)
  		this.#categoryMap.clear();
  		this.domain.forEach((cat, i) => this.#categoryMap.set(cat, i + 1));
  
  		// Bake and upload palette
  		const paletteData = bakePalette(this.#scheme, { reverse: this.#reverse });
  		this.#gpuCoordinator.writeColorPalette(this.#paletteSlot, paletteData);
  
  		// Build and upload config struct
  		const config = this.#buildColorConfig({
  			type: 0, // ORDINAL
  			paletteOffset: this.#paletteSlot * 32,
  			paletteSize: Math.min(this.domain.length, 32)
  		});
  		this.#gpuCoordinator.writeColorConfig(this.#configSlot, config);
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Get integer type code for scale type.
  	 */
  	#getScaleTypeInt() {
  		switch (this.#scaleType) {
  			case 'ordinal': return 0;
  			case 'sequential': return 1;
  			case 'diverging': return 2;
  			case 'cyclical': return 3;
  			default: return 1;
  		}
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Build the ColorConfig struct as ArrayBuffer.
  	 * Uses DataView for correct binary layout (mixed f32/u32).
  	 * 
  	 * MUST MATCH WGSL LAYOUT:
  	 * struct ColorConfig {
  	 *   domainMin: f32,      // byte 0
  	 *   domainMax: f32,      // byte 4
  	 *   rampOffset: u32,     // byte 8
  	 *   paletteOffset: u32,  // byte 12
  	 *   paletteSize: u32,    // byte 16
  	 *   scaleType: u32,      // byte 20
  	 *   _pad: vec2<u32>,     // byte 24, 28
  	 * }; // 32 bytes total
  	 */
  	#buildColorConfig(params) {
  		const buffer = new ArrayBuffer(32);
  		const view = new DataView(buffer);
  		const LE = true; // Little Endian (WebGPU standard)
  
  		// f32 fields
  		view.setFloat32(0, params.domainMin ?? 0, LE);
  		view.setFloat32(4, params.domainMax ?? 0, LE);
  
  		// u32 fields
  		view.setUint32(8, params.rampOffset ?? 0, LE);
  		view.setUint32(12, params.paletteOffset ?? 0, LE);
  		view.setUint32(16, params.paletteSize ?? 0, LE);
  		view.setUint32(20, params.type ?? 0, LE);
  
  		// bytes 24-31 are padding (left as 0)
  
  		return buffer;
  	}
  
  	// ============================================================
  	// DOMAIN UPDATES
  	// ============================================================
  
  	/**
  	 * Update the domain. Triggers GPU re-upload.
  	 * @param {Array} newDomain - New domain values
  	 */
  	setDomain(newDomain) {
  		if (!newDomain || newDomain.length === 0) return;
  
  		// Check if domain actually changed
  		const domainChanged =
  			newDomain.length !== this.domain.length ||
  			newDomain.some((v, i) => v !== this.domain[i]);
  
  		if (!domainChanged) return;
  
  		this.domain = [...newDomain];
  		this.#uploadToGPU();
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Update the scheme. Triggers GPU re-upload.
  	 * @param {string} newScheme - New scheme name
  	 */
  	setScheme(newScheme) {
  		if (newScheme === this.#scheme) return;
  
  		const newType = getSchemeType(newScheme);
  		const wasOrdinal = this.isOrdinal;
  		const nowOrdinal = newType === 'ordinal';
  
  		// Swap slots if changing between ordinal and continuous
  		if (wasOrdinal !== nowOrdinal) {
  			this.#swapSlots(nowOrdinal);
  		}
  
  		this.#scheme = newScheme;
  		this.#scaleType = newType || 'sequential';
  
  		if (this.domain.length > 0) {
  			this.#uploadToGPU();
  		}
  	}
  
  	// ============================================================
  	// MARK REGISTRATION (for domain aggregation)
  	// ============================================================
  
  	/**
  	 * Register a mark's data extent with this scale.
  	 * @param {Array} extent - [min, max] for continuous, or category array for ordinal
  	 * @param {string} markId - Unique mark identifier
  	 */
  	addMark(extent, markId) {
  		// For now, simple domain overwrite
  		// TODO: Aggregate across multiple marks if needed
  		this.setDomain(extent);
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Unregister a mark from this scale.
  	 * @param {string} markId - Unique mark identifier
  	 */
  	removeMark(markId) {
  		// TODO: Remove mark's contribution to domain
  	}
  
  	// ============================================================
  	// CPU-SIDE COLOR LOOKUP
  	// ============================================================
  
  	/**
  	 * Get color for a value (CPU-side lookup).
  	 * @param {*} value - Domain value
  	 * @returns {string} CSS color string
  	 */
  	getColor(value) {
  		if (!this.#d3Scale) return '#000000';
  		return this.#d3Scale(value);
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Get color as RGBA array (0-1 range).
  	 * @param {*} value - Domain value
  	 * @returns {[number, number, number, number]} RGBA array
  	 */
  	getColorRGBA(value) {
  		const cssColor = this.getColor(value);
  		return parseColorToRGBA(cssColor);
  	}
  
  	// ============================================================
  	// CLEANUP
  	// ============================================================
  
  	destroy() {
  		// Slots are released by registry via gpuSlots getter
  		this.#d3Scale = null;
  		this.#categoryMap.clear();
  		this.ready = false;
  	}
  }
</src/lib/scales/ColorScaleState.svelte.js>

<src/lib/scales/color-utils.js>
  // src/lib/scales/color-utils.js
  // Pure utility functions for color parsing and D3 scale creation
  
  import { color } from 'd3-color';
  import { scaleOrdinal, scaleSequential, scaleDiverging } from 'd3-scale';
  import {
    // Sequential single-hue
    interpolateBlues,
    interpolateGreens,
    interpolateGreys,
    interpolateOranges,
    interpolatePurples,
    interpolateReds,
    // Sequential multi-hue
    interpolateViridis,
    interpolateInferno,
    interpolateMagma,
    interpolatePlasma,
    interpolateCividis,
    interpolateTurbo,
    interpolateWarm,
    interpolateCool,
    interpolateCubehelixDefault,
    interpolateBuGn,
    interpolateBuPu,
    interpolateGnBu,
    interpolateOrRd,
    interpolatePuBuGn,
    interpolatePuBu,
    interpolatePuRd,
    interpolateRdPu,
    interpolateYlGnBu,
    interpolateYlGn,
    interpolateYlOrBr,
    interpolateYlOrRd,
    // Diverging
    interpolateBrBG,
    interpolatePRGn,
    interpolatePiYG,
    interpolatePuOr,
    interpolateRdBu,
    interpolateRdGy,
    interpolateRdYlBu,
    interpolateRdYlGn,
    interpolateSpectral,
    // Cyclical
    interpolateRainbow,
    interpolateSinebow,
    // Ordinal
    schemeCategory10,
    schemeAccent,
    schemeDark2,
    schemePaired,
    schemePastel1,
    schemePastel2,
    schemeSet1,
    schemeSet2,
    schemeSet3,
    schemeTableau10
  } from 'd3-scale-chromatic';
  
  /**
   * Set of valid CSS named colors for constant color detection.
   * Used by BaseMarkSetup to distinguish "red" (constant) from "price" (field).
   */
  export const CSS_COLOR_NAMES = new Set([
    'aliceblue', 'antiquewhite', 'aqua', 'aquamarine', 'azure',
    'beige', 'bisque', 'black', 'blanchedalmond', 'blue', 'blueviolet',
    'brown', 'burlywood', 'cadetblue', 'chartreuse', 'chocolate',
    'coral', 'cornflowerblue', 'cornsilk', 'crimson', 'cyan',
    'darkblue', 'darkcyan', 'darkgoldenrod', 'darkgray', 'darkgreen',
    'darkgrey', 'darkkhaki', 'darkmagenta', 'darkolivegreen', 'darkorange',
    'darkorchid', 'darkred', 'darksalmon', 'darkseagreen', 'darkslateblue',
    'darkslategray', 'darkslategrey', 'darkturquoise', 'darkviolet',
    'deeppink', 'deepskyblue', 'dimgray', 'dimgrey', 'dodgerblue',
    'firebrick', 'floralwhite', 'forestgreen', 'fuchsia', 'gainsboro',
    'ghostwhite', 'gold', 'goldenrod', 'gray', 'green', 'greenyellow',
    'grey', 'honeydew', 'hotpink', 'indianred', 'indigo', 'ivory',
    'khaki', 'lavender', 'lavenderblush', 'lawngreen', 'lemonchiffon',
    'lightblue', 'lightcoral', 'lightcyan', 'lightgoldenrodyellow',
    'lightgray', 'lightgreen', 'lightgrey', 'lightpink', 'lightsalmon',
    'lightseagreen', 'lightskyblue', 'lightslategray', 'lightslategrey',
    'lightsteelblue', 'lightyellow', 'lime', 'limegreen', 'linen',
    'magenta', 'maroon', 'mediumaquamarine', 'mediumblue', 'mediumorchid',
    'mediumpurple', 'mediumseagreen', 'mediumslateblue', 'mediumspringgreen',
    'mediumturquoise', 'mediumvioletred', 'midnightblue', 'mintcream',
    'mistyrose', 'moccasin', 'navajowhite', 'navy', 'oldlace', 'olive',
    'olivedrab', 'orange', 'orangered', 'orchid', 'palegoldenrod',
    'palegreen', 'paleturquoise', 'palevioletred', 'papayawhip',
    'peachpuff', 'peru', 'pink', 'plum', 'powderblue', 'purple',
    'rebeccapurple', 'red', 'rosybrown', 'royalblue', 'saddlebrown',
    'salmon', 'sandybrown', 'seagreen', 'seashell', 'sienna', 'silver',
    'skyblue', 'slateblue', 'slategray', 'slategrey', 'snow',
    'springgreen', 'steelblue', 'tan', 'teal', 'thistle', 'tomato',
    'turquoise', 'violet', 'wheat', 'white', 'whitesmoke', 'yellow',
    'yellowgreen', 'transparent'
  ]);
  
  // Pre-compiled regex for RGB parsing
  const RGB_REGEX = /rgba?\(\s*([0-9.]+%?)\s*[,\s]\s*([0-9.]+%?)\s*[,\s]\s*([0-9.]+%?)(?:\s*[,\/]\s*([0-9.]+%?))?\s*\)/;
  
  /**
   * Parse any CSS color string to [r, g, b, a] array with values 0-1.
   * Supports: hex (#rgb, #rrggbb, #rrggbbaa), rgb(), rgba(), hsl(), hsla(), named colors.
   * 
   * @param {string} colorStr - CSS color string
   * @returns {[number, number, number, number]} RGBA array with values 0-1
   */
  export function parseColorToRGBA(colorStr) {
    if (!colorStr || typeof colorStr !== 'string') {
      return [0, 0, 0, 1]; // Default to black
    }
  
    const trimmed = colorStr.trim().toLowerCase();
  
    // Handle hex colors
    if (trimmed.startsWith('#')) {
      return parseHexColor(trimmed);
    }
  
    // Handle rgb/rgba
    if (trimmed.startsWith('rgb')) {
      return parseRGBColor(trimmed);
    }
  
    // Handle hsl/hsla and named colors using D3
    const d3Color = color(trimmed);
    if (d3Color) {
      return [d3Color.r / 255, d3Color.g / 255, d3Color.b / 255, d3Color.opacity];
    }
  
    // Fallback to black
    console.warn(`Unable to parse color: ${colorStr}`);
    return [0, 0, 0, 1];
  }
  
  /**
   * Parse hex color string to RGBA array.
   */
  function parseHexColor(hex) {
    // Remove #
    let h = hex.slice(1);
  
    // Expand shorthand (#rgb → #rrggbb, #rgba → #rrggbbaa)
    if (h.length === 3) {
      h = h[0] + h[0] + h[1] + h[1] + h[2] + h[2];
    } else if (h.length === 4) {
      h = h[0] + h[0] + h[1] + h[1] + h[2] + h[2] + h[3] + h[3];
    }
  
    const r = parseInt(h.slice(0, 2), 16) / 255;
    const g = parseInt(h.slice(2, 4), 16) / 255;
    const b = parseInt(h.slice(4, 6), 16) / 255;
    const a = h.length === 8 ? parseInt(h.slice(6, 8), 16) / 255 : 1;
  
    return [r, g, b, a];
  }
  
  /**
   * Parse rgb() or rgba() color string to RGBA array.
   */
  function parseRGBColor(rgb) {
    const match = rgb.match(RGB_REGEX);
    
    if (!match) {
      return [0, 0, 0, 1];
    }
  
    const parseComponent = (val) => {
      if (val.endsWith('%')) {
        return parseFloat(val) / 100;
      }
      return parseFloat(val) / 255;
    };
  
    const parseAlpha = (val) => {
      if (!val) return 1;
      if (val.endsWith('%')) {
        return parseFloat(val) / 100;
      }
      return parseFloat(val);
    };
  
    return [
      parseComponent(match[1]),
      parseComponent(match[2]),
      parseComponent(match[3]),
      parseAlpha(match[4])
    ];
  }
  
  /**
   * D3 color scheme mappings.
   */
  const SEQUENTIAL_SCHEMES = {
    // Single-hue
    blues: interpolateBlues,
    greens: interpolateGreens,
    greys: interpolateGreys,
    oranges: interpolateOranges,
    purples: interpolatePurples,
    reds: interpolateReds,
    
    // Multi-hue
    viridis: interpolateViridis,
    inferno: interpolateInferno,
    magma: interpolateMagma,
    plasma: interpolatePlasma,
    cividis: interpolateCividis,
    turbo: interpolateTurbo,
    warm: interpolateWarm,
    cool: interpolateCool,
    cubehelixdefault: interpolateCubehelixDefault,
    
    // Multi-hue sequential
    bugn: interpolateBuGn,
    bupu: interpolateBuPu,
    gnbu: interpolateGnBu,
    orrd: interpolateOrRd,
    pubugn: interpolatePuBuGn,
    pubu: interpolatePuBu,
    purd: interpolatePuRd,
    rdpu: interpolateRdPu,
    ylgnbu: interpolateYlGnBu,
    ylgn: interpolateYlGn,
    ylorbr: interpolateYlOrBr,
    ylorrd: interpolateYlOrRd
  };
  
  const DIVERGING_SCHEMES = {
    brbg: interpolateBrBG,
    prgn: interpolatePRGn,
    piyg: interpolatePiYG,
    puor: interpolatePuOr,
    rdbu: interpolateRdBu,
    rdgy: interpolateRdGy,
    rdylbu: interpolateRdYlBu,
    rdylgn: interpolateRdYlGn,
    spectral: interpolateSpectral
  };
  
  const CYCLICAL_SCHEMES = {
    rainbow: interpolateRainbow,
    sinebow: interpolateSinebow
  };
  
  const ORDINAL_SCHEMES = {
    category10: schemeCategory10,
    accent: schemeAccent,
    dark2: schemeDark2,
    paired: schemePaired,
    pastel1: schemePastel1,
    pastel2: schemePastel2,
    set1: schemeSet1,
    set2: schemeSet2,
    set3: schemeSet3,
    tableau10: schemeTableau10
  };
  
  /**
   * Get the interpolator function for a scheme name.
   * Returns null if scheme is ordinal (discrete colors, not interpolatable).
   * 
   * @param {string} scheme - Scheme name (case-insensitive)
   * @returns {Function|null} D3 interpolator function or null
   */
  export function getInterpolator(scheme) {
    const key = scheme.toLowerCase();
    return SEQUENTIAL_SCHEMES[key] || DIVERGING_SCHEMES[key] || CYCLICAL_SCHEMES[key] || null;
  }
  
  /**
   * Get the color array for an ordinal scheme.
   * 
   * @param {string} scheme - Scheme name (case-insensitive)
   * @returns {string[]|null} Array of color strings or null
   */
  export function getOrdinalColors(scheme) {
    const key = scheme.toLowerCase();
    return ORDINAL_SCHEMES[key] || null;
  }
  
  /**
   * Determine if a scheme is ordinal (discrete) or continuous.
   * 
   * @param {string} scheme - Scheme name
   * @returns {'ordinal'|'sequential'|'diverging'|'cyclical'|null}
   */
  export function getSchemeType(scheme) {
    const key = scheme.toLowerCase();
    if (ORDINAL_SCHEMES[key]) return 'ordinal';
    if (SEQUENTIAL_SCHEMES[key]) return 'sequential';
    if (DIVERGING_SCHEMES[key]) return 'diverging';
    if (CYCLICAL_SCHEMES[key]) return 'cyclical';
    return null;
  }
  
  /**
   * Bake a configured D3 scale into a GPU-ready Float32Array.
   * This ensures domain asymmetry, midpoints, and clamping are correctly baked in.
   * 
   * The scale's domain logic is preserved: for diverging scales with asymmetric
   * domains (e.g., [-10, 0, 100]), the midpoint color will appear at the correct
   * texture coordinate.
   * 
   * @param {Function} d3Scale - Configured D3 scale (from createD3Scale)
   * @param {number} steps - Texture resolution (default 256)
   * @returns {Float32Array} steps*4 element array (steps colors × 4 components)
   */
  export function bakeGradient(d3Scale, steps = 256) {
    const result = new Float32Array(steps * 4);
    
    // Get the input domain from the scale itself
    const domain = d3Scale.domain();
    const min = domain[0];
    const max = domain[domain.length - 1];
  
    for (let i = 0; i < steps; i++) {
      // Map texture coordinate (0..1) back to data domain
      const t = i / (steps - 1);
      const value = min + t * (max - min);
      
      // Pass data value through d3Scale - this handles diverging logic, midpoints, etc.
      const c = color(d3Scale(value));
      
      const idx = i * 4;
      result[idx] = c.r / 255;
      result[idx + 1] = c.g / 255;
      result[idx + 2] = c.b / 255;
      result[idx + 3] = c.opacity;
    }
    
    return result;
  }
  
  /**
   * Bake an ordinal palette into a Float32Array for GPU upload.
   * Pads to exactly 32 colors (max palette size).
   * 
   * @param {string|string[]} schemeOrColors - Scheme name or array of color strings
   * @param {Object} options - Additional options
   * @param {boolean} options.reverse - Reverse the palette order
   * @returns {Float32Array} 128-element array (32 colors × 4 components)
   */
  export function bakePalette(schemeOrColors, options = {}) {
    const maxColors = 32;
    const result = new Float32Array(maxColors * 4);
    const { reverse = false } = options;
  
    let colors;
    if (typeof schemeOrColors === 'string') {
      colors = getOrdinalColors(schemeOrColors);
      if (!colors) {
        console.warn(`Unknown ordinal scheme: ${schemeOrColors}, falling back to tableau10`);
        colors = schemeTableau10;
      }
    } else {
      colors = schemeOrColors;
    }
  
    if (reverse) {
      colors = [...colors].reverse();
    }
  
    // Fill palette, cycling if needed for indices beyond color count
    for (let i = 0; i < maxColors; i++) {
      const c = color(colors[i % colors.length]);
      const idx = i * 4;
      result[idx] = c.r / 255;
      result[idx + 1] = c.g / 255;
      result[idx + 2] = c.b / 255;
      result[idx + 3] = c.opacity;
    }
  
    return result;
  }
  
  /**
   * Create a D3 scale for a color scheme.
   * Used for legends, CPU-side color lookups, AND as input to bakeGradient.
   * 
   * @param {string} scheme - Scheme name
   * @param {Array} domain - Domain values (2 elements for sequential, 3 for diverging with explicit midpoint)
   * @param {Object} options - Scale options
   * @param {boolean} options.reverse - Reverse the scale
   * @param {number} options.midpoint - Explicit midpoint for diverging scales (default: domain center)
   * @returns {Function} D3 scale function
   */
  export function createD3Scale(scheme, domain, options = {}) {
    const schemeType = getSchemeType(scheme);
    const { reverse = false, midpoint } = options;
  
    if (schemeType === 'ordinal') {
      let colors = getOrdinalColors(scheme);
      if (reverse) colors = [...colors].reverse();
      return scaleOrdinal().domain(domain).range(colors);
    }
  
    // Get interpolator, handle reverse
    let interpolator = getInterpolator(scheme);
    if (!interpolator) {
      console.warn(`Unknown scheme: ${scheme}, falling back to viridis`);
      interpolator = interpolateViridis;
    }
    
    if (reverse) {
      const original = interpolator;
      interpolator = (t) => original(1 - t);
    }
  
    // Diverging scales use scaleDiverging with 3-point domain
    if (schemeType === 'diverging') {
      const [min, max] = [domain[0], domain[domain.length - 1]];
      const mid = midpoint ?? (min + max) / 2;
      return scaleDiverging(interpolator).domain([min, mid, max]);
    }
  
    // Sequential and cyclical use scaleSequential
    return scaleSequential(interpolator).domain([domain[0], domain[domain.length - 1]]);
  }
  
  /**
   * Check if a string looks like a color value (constant) vs a field name.
   * 
   * @param {string} value - Value to check
   * @returns {boolean} True if value appears to be a color constant
   */
  export function isColorConstant(value) {
    if (!value || typeof value !== 'string') return false;
    
    const trimmed = value.trim().toLowerCase();
    
    // Hex color
    if (trimmed.startsWith('#')) return true;
    
    // RGB/RGBA/HSL/HSLA
    if (trimmed.startsWith('rgb') || trimmed.startsWith('hsl')) return true;
    
    // Named CSS color
    if (CSS_COLOR_NAMES.has(trimmed)) return true;
    
    return false;
  }
</src/lib/scales/color-utils.js>

<src/lib/animation/AnimationLoop.js>
  // =============================================================================
  // AnimationLoop.js - Fixed timestep animation loop with render extrapolation
  // =============================================================================
  
  import { DT } from './Spring.js';
  
  const MAX_DELTA = 0.1; // 100ms cap - handles tab switch, debugger pause, etc.
  
  export class AnimationLoop {
      #accumulator = 0;
      #lastTime = 0;
      #rafId = null;
      #isRunning = false;
  
      #onPhysicsStep;
      #onRender;
      #onIdleCheck;
  
      #frameCount = 0;
      #physicsStepsThisFrame = 0;
      #lastDelta = 0;
  
      #boundFrame;
  
      constructor({ onPhysicsStep, onRender, onIdleCheck = null }) {
          if (typeof onPhysicsStep !== 'function') {
              throw new Error('AnimationLoop requires onPhysicsStep callback');
          }
          if (typeof onRender !== 'function') {
              throw new Error('AnimationLoop requires onRender callback');
          }
  
          this.#onPhysicsStep = onPhysicsStep;
          this.#onRender = onRender;
          this.#onIdleCheck = onIdleCheck;
  
          this.#boundFrame = this.#frame.bind(this);
      }
  
      // ===========================================================================
      // PUBLIC API
      // ===========================================================================
  
      start() {
          if (this.#rafId !== null) return;
  
          this.#isRunning = true;
          this.#lastTime = performance.now();
          this.#accumulator = 0;
          this.#rafId = requestAnimationFrame(this.#boundFrame);
      }
  
      requestStart() {
          if (this.#rafId === null) {
              this.start();
          }
      }
  
      stop() {
          this.#isRunning = false;
          if (this.#rafId !== null) {
              cancelAnimationFrame(this.#rafId);
              this.#rafId = null;
          }
      }
  
      destroy() {
          this.stop();
      }
  
      // ===========================================================================
      // GETTERS
      // ===========================================================================
  
      get frameCount() {
          return this.#frameCount;
      }
  
      get physicsStepsLastFrame() {
          return this.#physicsStepsThisFrame;
      }
  
      get lastDelta() {
          return this.#lastDelta;
      }
  
      get accumulator() {
          return this.#accumulator;
      }
  
      get isRunning() {
          return this.#isRunning && this.#rafId !== null;
      }
  
      // ===========================================================================
      // FRAME LOOP
      // ===========================================================================
  
      #frame(time) {
        const now = time;
          // const now = performance.now();
          let delta = (now - this.#lastTime) / 1000;
          this.#lastTime = now;
  
          // Clamp delta - handles tab switch, debugger pause, slow frames
          if (delta > MAX_DELTA) {
              delta = MAX_DELTA;
          }
  
          this.#lastDelta = delta;
          this.#accumulator += delta;
  
          // Fixed timestep physics
          this.#physicsStepsThisFrame = 0;
          while (this.#accumulator >= DT) {
              this.#onPhysicsStep();
              this.#accumulator -= DT;
              this.#physicsStepsThisFrame++;
          }
  
          // Render with extrapolation
          this.#onRender(this.#accumulator);
  
          this.#frameCount++;
  
          // Check if we should continue
          const shouldContinue = this.#onIdleCheck ? !this.#onIdleCheck() : true;
  
          if (this.#isRunning && shouldContinue) {
              this.#rafId = requestAnimationFrame(this.#boundFrame);
          } else {
              this.#rafId = null;
          }
      }
  }
</src/lib/animation/AnimationLoop.js>

<src/lib/animation/Spring.js>
  // =============================================================================
  // Spring.js - Optimized spring physics with fixed timestep and render extrapolation
  // =============================================================================
  
  // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // PHYSICS CONSTANTS
  // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Fixed timestep for deterministic physics simulation.
  // 360Hz provides smooth results while being computationally efficient.
  // Higher rates (480Hz, 960Hz) offer marginal improvement at higher CPU cost.
  export const PHYSICS_HZ = 360;
  export const DT = 1 / PHYSICS_HZ;           // Fixed timestep in seconds (~2.78ms)
  export const DT_MS = DT * 1000;             // Fixed timestep in milliseconds
  
  // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // SPRING PRESETS
  // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Stiffness values tuned for different interaction types.
  // Higher stiffness = faster response, lower = more gradual
  export const SPRING_PRESETS = {
  	default: { stiffness: 400, precision: 1e-6 },
  	pan: { stiffness: 600, precision: 1e-6 },
  	zoom: { stiffness: 400, precision: 1e-6 },
  	reset: { stiffness: 150, precision: 1e-4 },
  	snap: { stiffness: 800, precision: 1e-6 }
  };
  
  // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // SPRING CLASS
  // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  /**
   * A critically-damped spring optimized for UI animations.
   *
   * Key features:
   * - Fixed timestep physics for deterministic behavior
   * - Precomputed exponential decay for hot-path performance
   * - Pure render extrapolation (no state mutation)
   * - Zero-allocation in animation loop
   *
   * Usage:
   * ```js
   * const spring = new Spring(0, SPRING_PRESETS.default.stiffness);
   *
   * // In physics loop (fixed timestep):
   * spring.step();
   *
   * // In render loop (variable timestep):
   * const renderValue = spring.getRenderable(remainingDt);
   * ```
   */
  export class Spring {
  	// Current physics state
  	current = 0;
  	target = 0;
  	velocity = 0;
  
  	// Precomputed constants (set via configure())
  	#omega = 0;      // Natural frequency: sqrt(stiffness)
  	#expDt = 0;      // Precomputed: exp(-omega * DT)
  	#precision = 1e-6;
  
  	/**
  	 * @param {number} initial - Initial value
  	 * @param {number} stiffness - Spring stiffness (default: 400)
  	 * @param {number} precision - Rest detection threshold (default: 1e-6)
  	 */
  	constructor(initial = 0, stiffness = 400, precision = 1e-6) {
  		this.current = initial;
  		this.target = initial;
  		this.#precision = precision;
  		this.configure(stiffness);
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Reconfigure spring stiffness. Call this when changing interaction modes.
  	 * Precomputes derived values for hot-path performance.
  	 *
  	 * @param {number} stiffness - New stiffness value
  	 */
  	configure(stiffness) {
  		this.#omega = Math.sqrt(stiffness);
  		this.#expDt = Math.exp(-this.#omega * DT);
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Advance physics by one fixed timestep.
  	 * Uses analytical solution for critically-damped spring.
  	 *
  	 * This is the "authoritative" state update - call at fixed rate (e.g., 360Hz).
  	 * Uses precomputed constants for maximum performance.
  	 */
  	step() {
  		const omega = this.#omega;
  		const expDt = this.#expDt;
  		const d = this.current - this.target;
  		const v = this.velocity;
  
  		// Analytical solution for critically-damped spring:
  		// x(t) = target + e^(-ωt) * (d + (v + ωd)t)
  		// v(t) = e^(-ωt) * (v + ωd) - ω * x(t)
  		const displacement = expDt * (d + (v + omega * d) * DT);
  		this.current = this.target + displacement;
  		this.velocity = displacement * -omega + expDt * (v + omega * d);
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Get interpolated render value without mutating state.
  	 * Call this once per frame with the remaining accumulator time.
  	 *
  	 * This provides smooth visuals even when physics rate differs from display rate.
  	 *
  	 * @param {number} dt - Time to extrapolate (in seconds), typically the accumulator remainder
  	 * @returns {number} Extrapolated position for rendering
  	 */
  	getRenderable(dt) {
  		const d = this.current - this.target;
  		const v = this.velocity;
  
  		// Early exit if at rest
  		if (d === 0 && v === 0) return this.current;
  
  		// Early exit if no extrapolation needed
  		if (dt === 0) return this.current;
  
  		// Same analytical solution, but for arbitrary dt (not precomputed)
  		const omega = this.#omega;
  		const exp = Math.exp(-omega * dt);
  		return this.target + exp * (d + (v + omega * d) * dt);
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * "Grab" the spring - used when starting a drag interaction.
  	 * Snaps target to current position and zeros velocity.
  	 * This prevents the spring from fighting the user's input.
  	 */
  	grab() {
  		this.target = this.current;
  		this.velocity = 0;
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Set target value. The spring will animate toward this value.
  	 *
  	 * @param {number} value - New target value
  	 */
  	setTarget(value) {
  		this.target = value;
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Instantly set both current and target to value, with zero velocity.
  	 * Use for initialization or hard resets (no animation).
  	 *
  	 * @param {number} value - Value to set
  	 */
  	set(value) {
  		this.current = value;
  		this.target = value;
  		this.velocity = 0;
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Check if spring is at rest (close enough to target with negligible velocity).
  	 * Used to determine when to stop animating.
  	 *
  	 * @param {number} [precision] - Optional override for precision threshold
  	 * @returns {boolean} True if spring is at rest
  	 */
  	isAtRest(precision) {
  		const p = precision ?? this.#precision;
  		return Math.abs(this.current - this.target) < p && Math.abs(this.velocity) < p;
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Get current omega (natural frequency). Useful for debugging.
  	 * @returns {number}
  	 */
  	get omega() {
  		return this.#omega;
  	}
  }
  
  // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // SPRING PAIR CLASS
  // -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  /**
   * A pair of springs for 2D values (e.g., domain min/max, x/y position).
   * Provides convenience methods for common operations.
   */
  export class SpringPair {
  	#springA;
  	#springB;
  
  	/**
  	 * @param {number} initialA - Initial value for first spring
  	 * @param {number} initialB - Initial value for second spring
  	 * @param {number} stiffness - Spring stiffness
  	 * @param {number} precision - Rest detection threshold
  	 */
  	constructor(initialA = 0, initialB = 0, stiffness = 400, precision = 1e-6) {
  		this.#springA = new Spring(initialA, stiffness, precision);
  		this.#springB = new Spring(initialB, stiffness, precision);
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Configure both springs with new stiffness.
  	 * @param {number} stiffness
  	 */
  	configure(stiffness) {
  		this.#springA.configure(stiffness);
  		this.#springB.configure(stiffness);
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Step both springs by one fixed timestep.
  	 */
  	step() {
  		this.#springA.step();
  		this.#springB.step();
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Get extrapolated render values for both springs.
  	 * @param {number} dt - Extrapolation time in seconds
  	 * @returns {{a: number, b: number}}
  	 */
  	getRenderable(dt) {
  		return {
  			a: this.#springA.getRenderable(dt),
  			b: this.#springB.getRenderable(dt)
  		};
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Get extrapolated render values into a provided array (zero-allocation).
  	 * @param {number} dt - Extrapolation time in seconds
  	 * @param {Float64Array|number[]} out - Output array [a, b]
  	 */
  	getRenderableInto(dt, out) {
  		out[0] = this.#springA.getRenderable(dt);
  		out[1] = this.#springB.getRenderable(dt);
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Grab both springs (for starting drag).
  	 */
  	grab() {
  		this.#springA.grab();
  		this.#springB.grab();
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Set targets for both springs.
  	 * @param {number} a - Target for first spring
  	 * @param {number} b - Target for second spring
  	 */
  	setTarget(a, b) {
  		this.#springA.target = a;
  		this.#springB.target = b;
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Instantly set both springs (no animation).
  	 * @param {number} a
  	 * @param {number} b
  	 */
  	set(a, b) {
  		this.#springA.set(a);
  		this.#springB.set(b);
  	}
  
  	/**
  	 * Check if both springs are at rest.
  	 * @param {number} [precision]
  	 * @returns {boolean}
  	 */
  	isAtRest(precision) {
  		return this.#springA.isAtRest(precision) && this.#springB.isAtRest(precision);
  	}
  
  	// Direct access to individual springs
  	get a() {
  		return this.#springA;
  	}
  	get b() {
  		return this.#springB;
  	}
  
  	// Convenience getters for current state
  	get currentA() {
  		return this.#springA.current;
  	}
  	get currentB() {
  		return this.#springB.current;
  	}
  	get targetA() {
  		return this.#springA.target;
  	}
  	get targetB() {
  		return this.#springB.target;
  	}
  }
</src/lib/animation/Spring.js>

<src/lib/scales/ChartScales.svelte.js>
  // src/lib/scales/ChartScales.svelte.js
  import { getContext } from 'svelte';
  import { getScaleState } from '$lib/scales';
  import { resolveScaleConfiguration, SCALE_PRIORITIES } from './ScalePriorities.js';
  
  export class ChartScales {
  	#scaleRegistry;
  	scaleRequirements = $state({
  		x: [],
  		y: []
  	});
  
  	// Use $state instead of $derived for scale instances
  	#xScaleInstance = $state(null);
  	#yScaleInstance = $state(null);
  
  	// Track previous scales for cleanup
  	#previousXScale = null;
  	#previousYScale = null;
  
  	active = $state(false);
  
  	// --- MEMORY POOLS ---
  	// Reusable objects to avoid garbage collection during "pointerdown"
  	_xPanContext = {
  		initialScreenPos: 0,
  		initialDomain: [0, 0],
  		screenToDataRatio: 0,
  		domainWidth: 0,
  		screenDimension: 0,
  		axis: 'x',
  		lastScreenPos: 0
  	};
  
  	_yPanContext = {
  		initialScreenPos: 0,
  		initialDomain: [0, 0],
  		screenToDataRatio: 0,
  		domainWidth: 0,
  		screenDimension: 0,
  		axis: 'y',
  		lastScreenPos: 0
  	};
  
  	constructor(config) {
  		this.config = config;
  		this.gpuCoordinator = config.gpuCoordinator;
  		this.#scaleRegistry = config.scaleRegistry;
  
  		if (!this.#scaleRegistry) {
  			console.error('ChartScales initialized without scaleRegistry!');
  		}
  
  		// Effect: Update scale instances when requirements or labels change
  		$effect(() => {
  			const xLabel = this.xLabel;
  			const yLabel = this.yLabel;
  			const xConfig = this.scaleConfigurations.x;
  			const yConfig = this.scaleConfigurations.y;
  
  			// TODO move outside effect?
  			// const scaleRegistry = getContext('scale-registry');
  
  			// Get what scales SHOULD be
  
  			const neededXScale = this.#scaleRegistry.getOrCreateScale(
  				xLabel,
  				xConfig?.type || 'linear',
  				xConfig?.config || {},
  				this.chartId
  			);
  
  			const neededYScale = this.#scaleRegistry.getOrCreateScale(
  				yLabel,
  				yConfig?.type || 'linear',
  				yConfig?.config || {},
  				this.chartId
  			);
  
  			// const neededXScale = getScaleState(
  			// 	xLabel,
  			// 	xConfig?.type || 'linear',
  			// 	xConfig?.config || {},
  			// 	// this.gpuCoordinator,
  			// 	this.chartId
  			// );
  
  			// const neededYScale = getScaleState(
  			// 	yLabel,
  			// 	yConfig?.type || 'linear',
  			// 	yConfig?.config || {},
  			// 	// this.gpuCoordinator,
  			// 	this.chartId
  			// );
  
  			// Check if X scale changed
  			if (this.#xScaleInstance !== neededXScale) {
  				// Cleanup old scale - REMOVE FROM THAT SCALE'S USAGE
  				if (this.#xScaleInstance) {
            this.#scaleRegistry.deregisterChart(this.#xScaleInstance, this.chartId);
  					// scaleRegistry?.deregisterChart(this.#xScaleInstance, this.chartId);
  				}
  				// const scaleRegistry = getContext('scale-registry');
  				// if (scaleRegistry && scaleRegistry.scaleUsage.has(this.#xScaleInstance)) {
  				// 	const usage = scaleRegistry.scaleUsage.get(this.#xScaleInstance);
  				// 	usage.delete(this.chartId);
  				// }
  				// Set new scale
  				this.#xScaleInstance = neededXScale;
  				this.#previousXScale = neededXScale;
  			}
  
  			// // Check if Y scale changed
  			// if (this.#yScaleInstance !== neededYScale) {
  			// 	// Cleanup old scale - REMOVE FROM THAT SCALE'S USAGE
  			// 	if (this.#yScaleInstance) {
  			// 		const scaleRegistry = getContext('scale-registry');
  			// 		if (scaleRegistry && scaleRegistry.scaleUsage.has(this.#yScaleInstance)) {
  			// 			const usage = scaleRegistry.scaleUsage.get(this.#yScaleInstance);
  			// 			usage.delete(this.chartId);
  			// 		}
  			// 	}
  
  			// 	// Set new scale
  			// 	this.#yScaleInstance = neededYScale;
  			// 	this.#previousYScale = neededYScale;
  			// }
  			// Check if Y scale changed
  			if (this.#yScaleInstance !== neededYScale) {
  				// 3. Use proper method for cleanup
  				if (this.#yScaleInstance) {
            this.#scaleRegistry.deregisterChart(this.#yScaleInstance, this.chartId);
  					// scaleRegistry?.deregisterChart(this.#yScaleInstance, this.chartId);
  				}
  
  				// Set new scale
  				this.#yScaleInstance = neededYScale;
  				this.#previousYScale = neededYScale;
  			}
  		});
  
  		// Effect: Notify GPU coordinator of scale mapping changes
  		$effect(() => {
  			const xScale = this.#xScaleInstance;
  			const yScale = this.#yScaleInstance;
  
  			if (xScale?.id && yScale?.id && this.gpuCoordinator) {
  				const trySetMapping = () => {
  					try {
  						this.gpuCoordinator.setChartScaleMapping(this.chartId, {
  							xScaleId: xScale.id,
  							yScaleId: yScale.id
  						});
  					} catch (error) {
  						// Retry logic for race conditions
  						setTimeout(trySetMapping, 10);
  					}
  				};
  				trySetMapping();
  			}
  		});
  	}
  
  	// Getters
  	get xLabel() {
  		return this.config.xLabel;
  	}
  
  	get yLabel() {
  		return this.config.yLabel;
  	}
  
  	get layout() {
  		return this.config.layout;
  	}
  
  	get chartId() {
  		return this.config.layout.chartId;
  	}
  
  	// Derived scale configurations
  	scaleConfigurations = $derived.by(() => {
  		try {
  			return {
  				x: resolveScaleConfiguration(this.scaleRequirements.x),
  				y: resolveScaleConfiguration(this.scaleRequirements.y)
  			};
  		} catch (error) {
  			console.error('Scale config error', error);
  			return { x: { type: 'linear', config: {} }, y: { type: 'linear', config: {} } };
  		}
  	});
  
  	scaleTypes = $derived.by(() => ({
  		x: this.scaleConfigurations?.x?.type || 'linear',
  		y: this.scaleConfigurations?.y?.type || 'linear'
  	}));
  
  	// Expose scale instances as getters (now using $state)
  	get xScaleState() {
  		return this.#xScaleInstance;
  	}
  
  	get yScaleState() {
  		return this.#yScaleInstance;
  	}
  
  	// Base D3 scales
  	baseXScale = $derived(
  		this.layout.isReady && this.#xScaleInstance
  			? this.#xScaleInstance.getBaseScale('x', this.layout.innerWidth)
  			: null
  	);
  
  	baseYScale = $derived(
  		this.layout.isReady && this.#yScaleInstance
  			? this.#yScaleInstance.getBaseScale('y', this.layout.innerHeight)
  			: null
  	);
  
  	// D3 scales with current domains
  	get xScale() {
  		if (!this.layout.isReady || !this.baseXScale || !this.#xScaleInstance) return null;
  		this.#xScaleInstance.domainForReactivity; // Register dependency
  		return this.#xScaleInstance.updateScaleDomain(this.baseXScale);
  	}
  
  	get yScale() {
  		if (!this.layout.isReady || !this.baseYScale || !this.#yScaleInstance) return null;
  		this.#yScaleInstance.domainForReactivity; // Register dependency
  		return this.#yScaleInstance.updateScaleDomain(this.baseYScale);
  	}
  
  	get xTargetScale() {
  		if (!this.layout.isReady || !this.baseXScale || !this.#xScaleInstance) return null;
  		return this.#xScaleInstance.updateScaleDomain(this.baseXScale, false);
  	}
  
  	get yTargetScale() {
  		if (!this.layout.isReady || !this.baseYScale || !this.#yScaleInstance) return null;
  		return this.#yScaleInstance.updateScaleDomain(this.baseYScale, false);
  	}
  
  	// Derived properties
  	currentXDataPoint = $derived(this.#xScaleInstance?.currentDataPoint);
  	currentYDataPoint = $derived(this.#yScaleInstance?.currentDataPoint);
  	currentXScreenPoint = $derived(this.xScale?.(this.currentXDataPoint));
  	currentYScreenPoint = $derived(this.yScale?.(this.currentYDataPoint));
  	scalesReady = $derived(this.#xScaleInstance?.ready && this.#yScaleInstance?.ready);
  
  	// Scale requirement registration
  	registerScaleType = (
  		dimension,
  		type,
  		priority = SCALE_PRIORITIES.PREFER,
  		markId = 'unknown',
  		config = {}
  	) => {
  		const requirements = this.scaleRequirements[dimension];
  		const existing = requirements.find(
  			(req) =>
  				req.type === type &&
  				req.priority === priority &&
  				req.markId === markId &&
  				JSON.stringify(req.config) === JSON.stringify(config)
  		);
  
  		if (!existing) {
  			requirements.push({ type, priority, markId, config });
  			this.scaleRequirements[dimension] = [...requirements];
  		}
  	};
  
  	unregisterScaleType = (dimension, markId) => {
  		const requirements = this.scaleRequirements[dimension];
  		const filtered = requirements.filter((req) => req.markId !== markId);
  		if (filtered.length !== requirements.length) {
  			this.scaleRequirements[dimension] = filtered;
  		}
  	};
  
  	getScaleRequirementsSummary = () => {
  		return {
  			x: this.scaleRequirements.x,
  			y: this.scaleRequirements.y,
  			resolvedTypes: this.scaleTypes
  		};
  	};
  
  	// Interaction methods
  	clearCurrentDataPoints = () => {
  		if (this.#xScaleInstance) this.#xScaleInstance.currentDataPoint = null;
  		if (this.#yScaleInstance) this.#yScaleInstance.currentDataPoint = null;
  	};
  
  	updateCurrentDataPoints = (innerPoint) => {
  		if (this.scaleTypes?.y === 'linear' && this.#yScaleInstance) {
  			this.#yScaleInstance.currentDataPoint = this.#yScaleInstance.invertScreenToData(
  				innerPoint.y,
  				this.layout.innerHeight,
  				true // isYAxis
  			);
  		}
  		if (this.scaleTypes?.x === 'linear' && this.#xScaleInstance) {
  			this.#xScaleInstance.currentDataPoint = this.#xScaleInstance.invertScreenToData(
  				innerPoint.x,
  				this.layout.innerWidth,
  				false
  			);
  		}
  	};
  
  	resetZoom = (resetX = true, resetY = true) => {
  		if (resetX && this.scaleTypes?.x === 'linear' && this.#xScaleInstance) {
  			this.#xScaleInstance.resetZoom();
  		}
  		if (resetY && this.scaleTypes?.y === 'linear' && this.#yScaleInstance) {
  			this.#yScaleInstance.resetZoom();
  		}
  	};
  
  	accumulateZoom = (innerPoint, scaleFactor, doX, doY) => {
  		if (doX && this.scaleTypes?.x === 'linear' && this.#xScaleInstance) {
  			const dataPoint = this.#xScaleInstance.invertScreenToData(
  				innerPoint.x,
  				this.layout.innerWidth,
  				false
  			);
  			this.#xScaleInstance.accumulateZoom(dataPoint, scaleFactor);
  		}
  		if (doY && this.scaleTypes?.y === 'linear' && this.#yScaleInstance) {
  			const dataPoint = this.#yScaleInstance.invertScreenToData(
  				innerPoint.y,
  				this.layout.innerHeight,
  				true
  			);
  			this.#yScaleInstance.accumulateZoom(dataPoint, scaleFactor);
  		}
  	};
  
  	applyZoom = (innerPoint, scaleFactor, doX, doY) => {
  		if (doX && this.scaleTypes?.x === 'linear' && this.#xScaleInstance) {
  			const dataPoint = this.#xScaleInstance.invertScreenToData(
  				innerPoint.x,
  				this.layout.innerWidth,
  				false
  			);
  			this.#xScaleInstance.accumulateZoom(dataPoint, scaleFactor);
  			this.#xScaleInstance.applyZoomDomain(dataPoint, scaleFactor, 'x', this.layout.innerWidth);
  		}
  		if (doY && this.scaleTypes?.y === 'linear' && this.#yScaleInstance) {
  			const dataPoint = this.#yScaleInstance.invertScreenToData(
  				innerPoint.y,
  				this.layout.innerHeight,
  				true
  			);
  			this.#yScaleInstance.accumulateZoom(dataPoint, scaleFactor);
  			this.#yScaleInstance.applyZoomDomain(dataPoint, scaleFactor, 'y', this.layout.innerHeight);
  		}
  	};
  
  	startPan = (x, y, activePanX, activePanY) => {
  		this.activePanX = activePanX;
  		this.activePanY = activePanY;
  
  		// X Axis Setup
  		if (activePanX && this.scaleTypes?.x === 'linear' && this.#xScaleInstance) {
  			const currentDomain = this.#xScaleInstance.domain.current;
  
  			this._xPanContext.initialScreenPos = x;
  			this._xPanContext.initialDomain[0] = currentDomain[0];
  			this._xPanContext.initialDomain[1] = currentDomain[1];
  			this._xPanContext.screenToDataRatio =
  				(currentDomain[1] - currentDomain[0]) / this.layout.innerWidth;
  			this._xPanContext.domainWidth = currentDomain[1] - currentDomain[0];
  			this._xPanContext.screenDimension = this.layout.innerWidth;
  			this._xPanContext.lastScreenPos = x;
  
  			this.#xScaleInstance.setPanContext(this._xPanContext);
  		}
  
  		// Y Axis Setup
  		if (activePanY && this.scaleTypes?.y === 'linear' && this.#yScaleInstance) {
  			const currentDomain = this.#yScaleInstance.domain.current;
  
  			this._yPanContext.initialScreenPos = y;
  			this._yPanContext.initialDomain[0] = currentDomain[0];
  			this._yPanContext.initialDomain[1] = currentDomain[1];
  			this._yPanContext.screenToDataRatio =
  				(currentDomain[1] - currentDomain[0]) / this.layout.innerHeight;
  			this._yPanContext.domainWidth = currentDomain[1] - currentDomain[0];
  			this._yPanContext.screenDimension = this.layout.innerHeight;
  			this._yPanContext.lastScreenPos = y;
  
  			this.#yScaleInstance.setPanContext(this._yPanContext);
  		}
  	};
  
  	endPan = () => {
  		this.activePanX = false;
  		this.activePanY = false;
  
  		if (this.scaleTypes?.x === 'linear' && this.#xScaleInstance) {
  			this.#xScaleInstance.endPan();
  		}
  		if (this.scaleTypes?.y === 'linear' && this.#yScaleInstance) {
  			this.#yScaleInstance.endPan();
  		}
  	};
  
  	updatePanPosition = (screenX, screenY) => {
  		if (this.activePanX && this.scaleTypes?.x === 'linear' && this.#xScaleInstance) {
  			this.#xScaleInstance.updatePanPosition(screenX, 0);
  		}
