  	//   @group(1) @binding(5) var<storage, read> alphaValues: array<f32>;
  
  	//   @vertex
  	//   fn vertexMain(
  	//     @location(0) rectCorner: vec2<f32>,
  	//     @builtin(instance_index) instanceIdx: u32,
  	//   ) -> VertexOutput {
  	//     let x0 = x0Values[instanceIdx];
  	//     let x1 = x1Values[instanceIdx];
  	//     let y0 = y0Values[instanceIdx];
  	//     let y1 = y1Values[instanceIdx];
  	//     let alpha = alphaValues[instanceIdx];
  	//     let color = colors[instanceIdx];
  
  	//     // Use centralized scale functions
  	//     let scaledX0 = scaleX(x0);
  	//     let scaledX1 = scaleX(x1);
  	//     let scaledY0 = scaleY(y0);
  	//     let scaledY1 = scaleY(y1);
  
  	//     // Interpolate rectangle corners
  	//     let finalX = mix(scaledX0, scaledX1, rectCorner.x);
  	//     let finalY = mix(scaledY0, scaledY1, rectCorner.y);
  
  	//     // Convert to normalized coordinates
  	//     let worldPos = vec2<f32>(finalX, finalY);
  	//     let normalizedPos = (worldPos / frameUniforms.resolution) * 2.0 - 1.0;
  
  	//     var output: VertexOutput;
  	//     output.position = vec4<f32>(normalizedPos.x, -normalizedPos.y, 0.0, 1.0);
  	//     output.color = vec4<f32>(color.rgb, color.a * alpha);
  	//     return output;
  	//   }
  
  	//   @fragment
  	//   fn fragmentMain(input: VertexOutput) -> @location(0) vec4<f32> {
  	//     return input.color;
  	//   }
  	// `;
  
  	const vertexBufferLayout = [
  		{
  			arrayStride: 2 * 4, // 2 floats * 4 bytes
  			attributes: [
  				{
  					shaderLocation: 0,
  					offset: 0,
  					format: 'float32x2'
  				}
  			]
  		}
  	];
  
  	const dataBindingConfig = [
  		{ binding: 0, type: 'read-only-storage' }, // x0Values
  		{ binding: 1, type: 'read-only-storage' }, // x1Values
  		{ binding: 2, type: 'read-only-storage' }, // y0Values
  		{ binding: 3, type: 'read-only-storage' }, // y1Values
  		{ binding: 4, type: 'read-only-storage' }, // colors
  		{ binding: 5, type: 'read-only-storage' } // alphaValues
  	];
  
  	// Buffer update effects
  	$effect(() => {
  		if (x0Data && renderer?.resourcesCreated) {
  			x0Buffer = renderer.createOrUpdateBuffer(x0Buffer, x0Data);
  		}
  	});
  
  	$effect(() => {
  		if (x1Data && renderer?.resourcesCreated) {
  			x1Buffer = renderer.createOrUpdateBuffer(x1Buffer, x1Data);
  		}
  	});
  
  	$effect(() => {
  		if (y0Data && renderer?.resourcesCreated) {
  			y0Buffer = renderer.createOrUpdateBuffer(y0Buffer, y0Data);
  		}
  	});
  
  	$effect(() => {
  		if (y1Data && renderer?.resourcesCreated) {
  			y1Buffer = renderer.createOrUpdateBuffer(y1Buffer, y1Data);
  		}
  	});
  
  	$effect(() => {
  		if (colorData && renderer?.resourcesCreated) {
  			colorBuffer = renderer.createOrUpdateBuffer(colorBuffer, colorData);
  		}
  	});
  
  	$effect(() => {
  		if (alphaData && renderer?.resourcesCreated) {
  			alphaBuffer = renderer.createOrUpdateBuffer(alphaBuffer, alphaData);
  		}
  	});
  
  	// Check for per-data bind group readiness
  	let dataBindGroupReady = $derived(!!renderer?.perDataBindGroup);
  
  	$effect(() => {
  		void renderer?.resourcesCreated;
  		void dataBindGroupReady;
  
  		if (
  			x0Buffer &&
  			x1Buffer &&
  			y0Buffer &&
  			y1Buffer &&
  			colorBuffer &&
  			alphaBuffer &&
  			renderer?.resourcesCreated &&
  			!dataBindGroupReady
  		) {
  			untrack(() => {
  				createRectsDataBindGroup();
  			});
  		}
  	});
  
  	// Data writing effects
  	$effect(() => {
  		void dataBindGroupReady;
  		if (x0Data && x0Buffer && dataBindGroupReady) {
  			untrack(() => {
  				const device = webGPUContext.getDevice();
  				device?.queue.writeBuffer(x0Buffer, 0, x0Data);
  			});
  		}
  	});
  
  	$effect(() => {
  		void dataBindGroupReady;
  		if (x1Data && x1Buffer && dataBindGroupReady) {
  			const device = webGPUContext.getDevice();
  			device?.queue.writeBuffer(x1Buffer, 0, x1Data);
  		}
  	});
  
  	$effect(() => {
  		void dataBindGroupReady;
  		if (y0Data && y0Buffer && dataBindGroupReady) {
  			const device = webGPUContext.getDevice();
  			device?.queue.writeBuffer(y0Buffer, 0, y0Data);
  		}
  	});
  
  	$effect(() => {
  		void dataBindGroupReady;
  
  		console.log(`📐 Rects received Y data:`, {
  			y0First5: Array.from(y0Data.slice(0, 5)),
  			y1First5: Array.from(y1Data.slice(0, 5)),
  			instanceCount: y0Data.length,
  			x0First5: Array.from(x0Data.slice(0, 5)),
  			x1First5: Array.from(x1Data.slice(0, 5)),
  			colorFirst5: colorData ? Array.from(colorData.slice(0, 12)) : 'null', // ← Add this (RGBA, so 4 values per instance)
  			alphaFirst3: alphaData ? Array.from(alphaData.slice(0, 3)) : 'null' // ← Add this
  		});
  
  		if (y1Data && y1Buffer && dataBindGroupReady) {
  			const device = webGPUContext.getDevice();
  			device?.queue.writeBuffer(y1Buffer, 0, y1Data);
  		}
  	});
  
  	$effect(() => {
  		void dataBindGroupReady;
  		if (colorData && colorBuffer && dataBindGroupReady) {
  			const device = webGPUContext.getDevice();
  			device?.queue.writeBuffer(colorBuffer, 0, colorData);
  		}
  	});
  
  	$effect(() => {
  		void dataBindGroupReady;
  		if (alphaData && alphaBuffer && dataBindGroupReady) {
  			const device = webGPUContext.getDevice();
  			device?.queue.writeBuffer(alphaBuffer, 0, alphaData);
  		}
  	});
  
  	$effect(() => {
  		void dataBindGroupReady;
  		if (
  			x0Data &&
  			x1Data &&
  			y0Data &&
  			y1Data &&
  			colorData &&
  			alphaData &&
  			x0Buffer &&
  			x1Buffer &&
  			y0Buffer &&
  			y1Buffer &&
  			colorBuffer &&
  			alphaBuffer &&
  			dataBindGroupReady
  		) {
  			renderer.updateInstanceCount(x0Data.length);
  			gpuCoordinator.invalidateChart(chartContext.chartId);
  		}
  	});
  
  	// Create unit square geometry for rectangle rendering
  	function createRectGeometry() {
  		return new Float32Array([
  			0,
  			0, // bottom-left  (x0, y0)
  			1,
  			0, // bottom-right (x1, y0)
  			0,
  			1, // top-left     (x0, y1)
  			1,
  			0, // bottom-right (x1, y0)
  			1,
  			1, // top-right    (x1, y1)
  			0,
  			1 // top-left     (x0, y1)
  		]);
  	}
  
  	async function initializeRectGeometry() {
  		try {
  			const device = webGPUContext.getDevice();
  			if (!device) {
  				throw new Error('No WebGPU device available');
  			}
  
  			// Create unit square geometry buffer
  			const rectVertices = createRectGeometry();
  			console.log('📐 Rect vertices:', rectVertices);
  
  			rectGeometryBuffer = device.createBuffer({
  				size: rectVertices.byteLength,
  				usage: GPUBufferUsage.VERTEX | GPUBufferUsage.COPY_DST,
  				label: 'rects-unit-square-geometry'
  			});
  			device.queue.writeBuffer(rectGeometryBuffer, 0, rectVertices);
  
  			console.log('✅ Rect geometry created successfully');
  		} catch (error) {
  			console.error('❌ Failed to create rect geometry:', error);
  			throw error;
  		}
  	}
  
  	function createRectsDataBindGroup() {
  		if (!x0Buffer || !x1Buffer || !y0Buffer || !y1Buffer || !colorBuffer || !alphaBuffer) {
  			return;
  		}
  
  		const dataBindings = [
  			{ binding: 0, resource: { buffer: x0Buffer } },
  			{ binding: 1, resource: { buffer: x1Buffer } },
  			{ binding: 2, resource: { buffer: y0Buffer } },
  			{ binding: 3, resource: { buffer: y1Buffer } },
  			{ binding: 4, resource: { buffer: colorBuffer } },
  			{ binding: 5, resource: { buffer: alphaBuffer } }
  		];
  
  		renderer.createDataBindGroup(dataBindings);
  	}
  
  	function drawRects(passEncoder) {
  		try {
  			if (!rectGeometryBuffer || renderer.instanceCount === 0) {
  				return;
  			}
  
  			passEncoder.setVertexBuffer(0, rectGeometryBuffer);
  			passEncoder.draw(rectVertexCount, renderer.instanceCount);
  		} catch (error) {
  			console.error('❌ Rects draw operation failed:', error);
  			throw error;
  		}
  	}
  
  	// Wait for WebGPU context
  	async function waitForWebGPU() {
  		let attempts = 0;
  		const maxAttempts = 100;
  
  		while (!webGPUContext.isInitialized() && attempts < maxAttempts) {
  			await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 50));
  			attempts++;
  		}
  
  		if (!webGPUContext.isInitialized()) {
  			throw new Error(`WebGPU context failed to initialize within ${maxAttempts * 50}ms`);
  		}
  
  		await initializeRects();
  	}
  
  	// // Initialize rectangles renderer
  	// async function initializeRects() {
  	// 	try {
  	// 		if (!gpuCoordinator) {
  	// 			throw new Error('GPU Coordinator context missing. Make sure setCoordinators() was called.');
  	// 		}
  
  	// 		// Create base renderer with scale type information
  	// 		renderer = new BaseGPURenderer('rects', webGPUContext, gpuCoordinator, chartContext, {
  	// 			zIndex,
  	// 			xScaleType: chartContext.scaleTypes?.x || 'linear',
  	// 			yScaleType: chartContext.scaleTypes?.y || 'linear'
  	// 		});
  
  	// 		await renderer.initialize({
  	// 			markShaderCode: rectsShaderCode,
  	// 			vertexBufferLayout,
  	// 			dataBindingConfig
  	// 		});
  
  	// 		// Initialize rectangle-specific geometry
  	// 		await initializeRectGeometry();
  
  	// 		// Register layer with base renderer
  	// 		renderer.registerLayer(drawRects);
  
  	// 		console.log('✅ Rects component initialized');
  	// 	} catch (error) {
  	// 		console.error('❌ Rects component initialization failed:', error);
  	// 		initializationError = error.message;
  	// 		throw error;
  	// 	}
  	// }
  
  	// Initialize rectangles renderer
  	async function initializeRects() {
  		try {
  			if (!gpuCoordinator) {
  				throw new Error('GPU Coordinator context missing. Make sure setCoordinators() was called.');
  			}
  
  			// Create base renderer with scale type information
  			renderer = new BaseGPURenderer('rects', webGPUContext, gpuCoordinator, chartContext, {
  				zIndex,
  				xScaleType: chartContext.scaleTypes?.x || 'linear',
  				yScaleType: chartContext.scaleTypes?.y || 'linear'
  			});
  
  			await renderer.initialize({
  				markShaderCode: rectsShaderCode,
  				vertexBufferLayout,
  				dataBindingConfig,
  				rendererDefinition: rectRendererDefinition // Pass the definition
  			});
  
  			// Initialize rectangle-specific geometry
  			await initializeRectGeometry();
  
  			// Register layer with base renderer
  			renderer.registerLayer(drawRects);
  
  			console.log('✅ Rects component initialized');
  		} catch (error) {
  			console.error('❌ Rects component initialization failed:', error);
  			initializationError = error.message;
  			throw error;
  		}
  	}
  
  	onMount(async () => {
  		try {
  			await waitForWebGPU();
  		} catch (error) {
  			console.error('❌ Rects component mount failed:', error);
  			initializationError = error.message;
  		}
  	});
  
  	onDestroy(() => {
  		try {
  			// Clean up LOCALLY OWNED geometry buffer
  			// This buffer is created directly in initializeRectGeometry(),
  			// NOT via renderer.createOrUpdateBuffer(), so we must destroy it manually.
  			if (rectGeometryBuffer) {
  				rectGeometryBuffer.destroy();
  				rectGeometryBuffer = null;
  			}
  
  			// Data buffers (x0Buffer, x1Buffer, y0Buffer, y1Buffer, colorBuffer, alphaBuffer)
  			// are created via renderer.createOrUpdateBuffer() and tracked in
  			// renderer.#ownedResources.buffers - they are automatically destroyed
  			// by renderer.destroy(). DO NOT destroy them here (would double-destroy).
  
  			// Base renderer handles:
  			// - Data buffer cleanup (tracked in #ownedResources)
  			// - Shared resource cleanup (via coordinator.releaseRenderer)
  			// - Layer unregistration
  			if (renderer) {
  				renderer.destroy();
  				renderer = null;
  			}
  		} catch (error) {
  			console.error('❌ Error during Rects component cleanup:', error);
  		}
  	});
  </script>
  
  {#if initializationError}
  	<div class="rects-error">
  		<p>Rects component failed to initialize:</p>
  		<p class="error-message">{initializationError}</p>
  	</div>
  {/if}
  
  <style>
  	.rects-error {
  		position: absolute;
  		top: 10px;
  		left: 10px;
  		background-color: rgba(255, 240, 240, 0.9);
  		border: 1px solid #ffcccb;
  		border-radius: 4px;
  		padding: 8px 12px;
  		font-size: 12px;
  		color: #d32f2f;
  		z-index: 1000;
  		max-width: 300px;
  	}
  
  	.error-message {
  		font-family: monospace;
  		background-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.8);
  		padding: 4px 6px;
  		border-radius: 2px;
  		margin-top: 4px;
  		font-size: 11px;
  	}
  </style>
</src/lib/components/rendering/Rects.svelte>

<src/lib/components/marks/BaseMarkSetup.svelte.js>
  // src/lib/components/marks/BaseMarkSetup.svelte.js
  
  import { untrack } from 'svelte';
  import { DataClient } from '$lib/data-clients/DataClient.svelte.js';
  import {
  	Query,
  	isNode,
  	column as sqlColumn,
  	isAggregateExpression,
  	isNotNull,
  	and
  } from '@uwdata/mosaic-sql';
  import { SCALE_PRIORITIES } from '$lib/scales/ScalePriorities.js';
  import { TransformCompiler } from '$lib/transforms/TransformCompiler.js';
  import { ArrowDataAccessor, ObjectDataAccessor } from '$lib/data/DataAccessor.js';
  
  export class BaseMarkSetup {
  	// --- Reactive State ---
  	client = $state(null);
  	categoricalDataFetched = $state(false);
  
  	// --- Setup Progress ---
  	#scaleStatesReady = $state(false);
  	#scaleMappingsReady = $state(false);
  	#clientCreated = $state(false);
  	#clientDataReady = $state(false);
  	#continuousDomainsSet = $state(false);
  	#categoricalFetchStarted = $state(false);
  
  	// --- Private Properties ---
  	#config;
  	#coordinator;
  	#chartContext;
  	#markId;
  	#scaleMap = {};
  
  	// --- Transform Support ---
  	#transformCompiler;
  	#autoBinNodes = new Map(); // channel -> AutoBinNode
  
  	constructor(config, coordinator, chartContext, stableId = null) {
  		console.log('🔧 BaseMarkSetup constructor:', {
  			markType: config.markType,
  			transforms: config.transforms,
  			transformCount: config.transforms?.length || 0
  		});
  
  		this.#config = config;
  		this.#coordinator = coordinator;
  		this.#chartContext = chartContext;
  		this.#markId = stableId || `${config.markType}-${crypto.randomUUID().slice(0, 8)}`;
  
  		// NEW: Create transform compiler
  		this.#transformCompiler = new TransformCompiler(config.transforms || []);
  
  		console.log('🔧 TransformCompiler created:', {
  			sqlTransforms: this.#transformCompiler.sqlTransforms.length,
  			jsTransforms: this.#transformCompiler.jsTransforms.length
  		});
  
  		// NEW: Detect auto bins in select
  		this.#detectAutoBins();
  
  		this.#registerScaleRequirements();
  		this.#setupReactiveEffects();
  	}
  
  	// NEW: Detect AutoBinNodes in config
  	#detectAutoBins() {
  		for (const [channel, value] of Object.entries(this.#config.select)) {
  			if (value?.isAutoBinNode) {
  				this.#autoBinNodes.set(channel, value);
  			}
  		}
  	}
  
  	#setupReactiveEffects() {
  		// Effect 1: Wait for scale objects
  		$effect(() => {
  			const { scaleTypes, xScaleState, yScaleState } = this.#chartContext;
  			if (!!(scaleTypes && xScaleState && yScaleState) && !this.#scaleStatesReady) {
  				this.#scaleStatesReady = true;
  			}
  		});
  
  		// Effect 2: Setup scale mappings
  		$effect(() => {
  			if (this.#scaleStatesReady && !this.#scaleMappingsReady) {
  				this.#setupScaleMappings();
  				this.#scaleMappingsReady = true;
  			}
  		});
  
  		// Effect 3: Create client
  		$effect(() => {
  			if (this.#scaleMappingsReady && !this.#clientCreated) {
  				const fieldRequests = this.#buildFieldInfoRequests();
  				this.#createClient(fieldRequests);
  				this.#clientCreated = true;
  			}
  		});
  
  		// Effect 4: Monitor client data readiness
  		$effect(() => {
  			if (this.#clientCreated && this.client?.fieldInfo && !this.#clientDataReady) {
  				this.#clientDataReady = true;
  			}
  		});
  
  		// Effect 5: Set initial continuous domains
  		$effect(() => {
  			if (this.#clientDataReady && !this.#continuousDomainsSet) {
  				this.#setInitialContinuousDomains(this.client.fieldInfo);
  				this.#continuousDomainsSet = true;
  			}
  		});
  
  		// Effect 6: Fetch categorical domains
  		$effect(() => {
  			if (this.#continuousDomainsSet && !this.#categoricalFetchStarted) {
  				this.#categoricalFetchStarted = true;
  
  				this.#fetchCategoricalDomains()
  					.then(() => {
  						this.categoricalDataFetched = true;
  						this.#setScalesReady();
  						console.log(`✅ Mark ${this.#markId} setup complete`);
  					})
  					.catch((error) => {
  						console.error(`❌ Categorical domain fetch failed for ${this.#markId}:`, error);
  					});
  			}
  		});
  	}
  
  	#registerScaleRequirements() {
  		const { scaleRequirements } = this.#config;
  
  		if (scaleRequirements?.x) {
  			this.#chartContext.registerScaleType(
  				'x',
  				scaleRequirements.x.type,
  				scaleRequirements.x.priority,
  				this.#markId
  			);
  		}
  
  		if (scaleRequirements?.y) {
  			this.#chartContext.registerScaleType(
  				'y',
  				scaleRequirements.y.type,
  				scaleRequirements.y.priority,
  				this.#markId
  			);
  		}
  	}
  
  	get actualXScaleType() {
  		return this.#chartContext.scaleTypes?.x || 'linear';
  	}
  
  	get actualYScaleType() {
  		return this.#chartContext.scaleTypes?.y || 'linear';
  	}
  
  	get markId() {
  		return this.#markId;
  	}
  
  	scaleCompatibilityError = $derived.by(() => {
  		const { supportedScaleTypes, markType } = this.#config;
  		if (!this.actualXScaleType || !this.actualYScaleType) return null;
  
  		const incompatibleX = !supportedScaleTypes.x.includes(this.actualXScaleType);
  		const incompatibleY = !supportedScaleTypes.y.includes(this.actualYScaleType);
  
  		if (incompatibleX)
  			return `${markType} cannot use scale type '${this.actualXScaleType}' for the x-axis.`;
  		if (incompatibleY)
  			return `${markType} cannot use scale type '${this.actualYScaleType}' for the y-axis.`;
  
  		return null;
  	});
  
  	gpuReady = $derived.by(() => {
  		if (!this.#chartContext.scalesReady || !this.categoricalDataFetched) return false;
  
  		const xReady =
  			this.actualXScaleType === 'band' || this.actualXScaleType === 'point'
  				? this.#chartContext.xScaleState?.isReadyForGPU
  				: this.#chartContext.xScaleState?.ready;
  
  		const yReady =
  			this.actualYScaleType === 'band' || this.actualYScaleType === 'point'
  				? this.#chartContext.yScaleState?.isReadyForGPU
  				: this.#chartContext.yScaleState?.ready;
  
  		return xReady && yReady;
  	});
  
  	// Processed data with JS transforms applied
  	// processedData = $derived.by(() => {
  	// 	if (!this.client?.arrowTable) return null;
  
  	// 	// Convert Arrow to objects
  	// 	const data = this.#arrowToObjects(this.client.arrowTable);
  
  	// 	// Apply JS transforms if any
  	// 	if (this.#transformCompiler.hasJSTransforms()) {
  	// 		return this.#transformCompiler.applyToData(
  	// 			data,
  	// 			{
  	// 				x: this.#getFieldName('x'),
  	// 				y: this.#getFieldName('y'),
  	// 				fill: this.#getFieldName('fill')
  	// 			},
  	// 			{
  	// 				x: this.#chartContext.xScale,
  	// 				y: this.#chartContext.yScale
  	// 			}
  	// 		);
  	// 	}
  
  	// 	return data;
  	// });
  	data = $derived.by(() => {
  		if (!this.client?.arrowTable) return null;
  
  		// Fast path: No JS transforms - return Arrow accessor (zero-copy)
  		if (!this.#transformCompiler.hasJSTransforms()) {
        console.log('📊 Using ArrowDataAccessor (fast path)');
  			return new ArrowDataAccessor(this.client.arrowTable);
  		}
  
  		// Transform path: Arrow → Objects → Transform → Object accessor
      console.log('📊 Using ObjectDataAccessor (JS transforms)');
  		const objects = this.#arrowToObjects(this.client.arrowTable);
  		const transformed = this.#transformCompiler.applyToData(
  			objects,
  			{
  				x: this.#getFieldName('x'),
  				y: this.#getFieldName('y'),
  				fill: this.#getFieldName('fill')
  			},
  			{
  				x: this.#chartContext.xScale,
  				y: this.#chartContext.yScale
  			}
  		);
  
      console.log('📊 Transformed data:', transformed.length, 'rows');
  
  		return new ObjectDataAccessor(transformed);
  	});
  
  	#arrowToObjects(arrowTable) {
  		const rows = [];
  		const numRows = arrowTable.numRows;
  
  		for (let i = 0; i < numRows; i++) {
  			const row = {};
  			for (const field of arrowTable.schema.fields) {
  				const column = arrowTable.getChild(field.name);
  				row[field.name] = column.get(i);
  			}
  			rows.push(row);
  		}
  
  		return rows;
  	}
  
  	#getFieldName(channel) {
  		const value = this.#config.select[channel];
  		if (typeof value === 'string') return value;
  		if (value?.field) return value.field; // AutoBinNode
  		return channel; // fallback
  	}
  
  	getSelectConfig() {
  		// Return select with AutoBinNodes unresolved
  		// They'll be resolved in buildQuery()
  		return this.#config.select;
  	}
  
  	buildQuery(sourceTable, config, filter) {
  		console.log('🔨 buildQuery called with filter:', filter);
  		console.log('🔨 buildQuery config:', config);
  		let select = config.select;
  
  		// NEW: Resolve auto bins if we have field info
  		if (this.#autoBinNodes.size > 0 && this.client?.fieldInfo) {
  			select = this.#resolveAutoBins(config.select);
  		}
  
  		const q = Query.from(sourceTable);
  		const selectDef = {};
  		const nonAggregateGroupBy = [];
  		let hasAggregates = false;
  
  		// Build select clause
  		for (const alias in select) {
  			const expr = select[alias];
  			if (expr == null) continue;
  
  			const exprNode = isNode(expr) ? expr : sqlColumn(String(expr));
  			selectDef[alias] = exprNode;
  
  			if (isAggregateExpression(exprNode)) {
  				hasAggregates = true;
  			} else {
  				nonAggregateGroupBy.push(exprNode);
  			}
  		}
  		q.select(selectDef);
  
  		// Add GROUP BY if needed
  		if (hasAggregates && nonAggregateGroupBy.length > 0) {
  			q.groupby(nonAggregateGroupBy);
  		}
  
  		// NEW: Apply SQL transforms
  		let queryWithTransforms = q;
  		if (this.#transformCompiler.hasSQLTransforms()) {
  			console.log('🔨 Applying SQL transforms...');
  			console.log('🔨 Query BEFORE transforms:', q.toString());
  			queryWithTransforms = this.#transformCompiler.applyToQuery(q);
  
  			console.log('🔨 Query AFTER transforms:', queryWithTransforms.toString());
  
  			// Validate query is still flat
  			this.#transformCompiler.validateQuery(queryWithTransforms);
  		}
  
  		// Add filters
  		const allFilters = [...filter];
  		if (config._filterNulls && Array.isArray(config._filterNulls)) {
  			allFilters.push(...config._filterNulls.map((col) => isNotNull(col)));
  		}
  		if (allFilters.length > 0) {
  			queryWithTransforms = queryWithTransforms.where(and(allFilters));
  		}
  
  		return queryWithTransforms;
  	}
  
  	// NEW: Resolve AutoBinNodes to concrete SQL
  	#resolveAutoBins(select) {
  		const resolved = {};
  
  		for (const [channel, value] of Object.entries(select)) {
  			if (value?.isAutoBinNode) {
  				const scaleType =
  					channel === 'x' ? this.actualXScaleType : channel === 'y' ? this.actualYScaleType : null;
  
  				resolved[channel] = value.resolve(this.client.fieldInfo, scaleType);
  			} else {
  				resolved[channel] = value;
  			}
  		}
  
  		return resolved;
  	}
  
  	#createClient(fieldRequests) {
  		if (this.client) this.client.destroy();
  
  		this.client = new DataClient(
  			this.#config.table,
  			this.#config.selection,
  			{
  				select: this.getSelectConfig(),
  				queryBuilder: (table, config, filter) => this.buildQuery(table, config, filter),
  				dataFormat: 'arrow',
  				name: this.#markId,
  				...this.#config.clientOptions
  			},
  			fieldRequests
  		);
  		this.#coordinator.connect(this.client);
  	}
  
  	#setupScaleMappings() {
  		this.#scaleMap = {
  			x: this.#chartContext.xScaleState,
  			y: this.#chartContext.yScaleState
  		};
  
  		console.log(`📊 ${this.#markId} Scale mappings setup:`, {
  			x: this.actualXScaleType,
  			y: this.actualYScaleType
  		});
  	}
  
  	#buildFieldInfoRequests() {
  		const { select, table } = this.#config;
  		const requests = [];
  		const processedFields = new Set();
  
  		for (const alias in select) {
  			const fieldValue = select[alias];
  			if (fieldValue == null) continue;
  
  			// NEW: Handle AutoBinNodes
  			if (fieldValue.isAutoBinNode) {
  				const stats = fieldValue.stats;
  				if (!processedFields.has(stats.column)) {
  					requests.push({ table, column: stats.column, stats: stats.stats });
  					processedFields.add(stats.column);
  				}
  				continue;
  			}
  
  			// Handle regular fields
  			if (processedFields.has(fieldValue.toString())) continue;
  
  			const exprNode = isNode(fieldValue) ? fieldValue : sqlColumn(String(fieldValue));
  			if (isAggregateExpression(exprNode)) continue;
  
  			const scaleType =
  				alias === 'x' ? this.actualXScaleType : alias === 'y' ? this.actualYScaleType : null;
  
  			if (scaleType === 'linear' || scaleType === 'time' || scaleType === 'log') {
  				requests.push({ table, column: exprNode, stats: ['min', 'max'] });
  				processedFields.add(fieldValue.toString());
  			}
  		}
  
  		return requests;
  	}
  
  	#setInitialContinuousDomains(fieldInfo) {
  		if (!fieldInfo) return;
  
  		for (const alias in this.#scaleMap) {
  			const scaleState = this.#scaleMap[alias];
  			if (!scaleState) continue;
  
  			const scaleType = alias === 'x' ? this.actualXScaleType : this.actualYScaleType;
  
  			if (scaleType === 'linear' || scaleType === 'time' || scaleType === 'log') {
  				const fieldValue = this.#config.select[alias];
  
  				// Skip AutoBinNodes - they handle their own stats
  				if (fieldValue?.isAutoBinNode) continue;
  
  				if (fieldValue == null) continue;
  
  				const infoKey = String(fieldValue).replace(/^["']|["']$/g, '');
  				const info = fieldInfo[infoKey];
  
  				if (info && info.min != null && info.max != null) {
  					scaleState.addMark([info.min, info.max], this.#markId);
  				}
  			}
  		}
  	}
  
  	async #fetchCategoricalDomains() {
  		const { table, selection, sort } = this.#config;
  		if (!this.#coordinator || !table) return;
  
  		const fetches = [];
  
  		const setupFetch = (axis, field) => {
  			if (!field) return;
  			const scaleState = this.#chartContext[`${axis}ScaleState`];
  			let q = Query.from(table).select({ [axis]: sqlColumn(String(field)) });
  
  			const sortConfig = sort;
  			if (sortConfig && sortConfig.by) {
  				const isSortingByOtherAxis = sortConfig.by === (axis === 'x' ? 'y' : 'x');
  				const otherAxisField = this.#config.select[sortConfig.by];
  				if (isSortingByOtherAxis && isAggregateExpression(otherAxisField)) {
  					q = q.groupby(field).select({ [sortConfig.by]: otherAxisField });
  				}
  				q.orderby({ [sortConfig.by]: sortConfig.direction === 'desc' ? 'DESC' : 'ASC' });
  			} else {
  				q.distinct();
  			}
  
  			fetches.push(
  				this.#coordinator.query(q).then((result) => {
  					const categories = Array.from(result).map((row) => row[axis]);
  					if (categories.length > 0) {
  						scaleState.addMark(categories, this.#markId);
  					}
  				})
  			);
  		};
  
  		if (this.actualXScaleType === 'band' || this.actualXScaleType === 'point') {
  			setupFetch('x', this.#config.select.x);
  		}
  		if (this.actualYScaleType === 'band' || this.actualYScaleType === 'point') {
  			setupFetch('y', this.#config.select.y);
  		}
  
  		if (fetches.length > 0) await Promise.all(fetches);
  	}
  
  	#setScalesReady() {
  		Object.values(this.#scaleMap).forEach((scale) => scale?.setReady?.());
  	}
  
  	destroy() {
  		console.log(`💥 BaseMarkSetup destroying for ${this.#markId}`);
  
  		this.#chartContext.unregisterScaleType('x', this.#markId);
  		this.#chartContext.unregisterScaleType('y', this.#markId);
  
  		Object.values(this.#scaleMap).forEach((scale) => scale?.removeMark?.(this.#markId));
  
  		if (this.client) {
  			this.client.destroy();
  			this.client = null;
  		}
  	}
  }
</src/lib/components/marks/BaseMarkSetup.svelte.js>

<src/lib/components/marks/DotsMark.svelte>
  <script>
  	import { getContext, onDestroy, untrack } from 'svelte';
  	import { BaseMarkSetup } from './BaseMarkSetup.svelte.js';
  	import { Dots } from '../rendering/index.js';
  	import { SCALE_PRIORITIES } from '$lib/scales/ScalePriorities';
  	import { isColorConstant, parseColorToRGBA } from '$lib/scales/color-utils.js';
  
  	let {
  		table,
  		selection,
  		x,
  		y,
  		color = null,        // Can be: null, "#ff0000", "red", "fieldName", { field, scheme }
  		scheme = 'tableau10', // Default scheme for field-based color
  		alpha = 0.5,
  		radius = 3,
  		limit,
  		filterNulls = false,
  		preferredXScaleType = 'linear',
  		preferredYScaleType = 'linear',
  		transforms = []
  	} = $props();
  
  	const markId = `dot-${crypto.randomUUID().slice(0, 8)}`;
  	const coordinator = getContext('mosaic-coordinator');
  	const chartContext = getContext('chart');
  	const scaleRegistry = getContext('scale-registry');
  
  	let setup = $state(null);
  	let colorScaleState = $state(null);
  
  	// Detect color type
  	const colorConfig = $derived.by(() => {
  		// No color prop → constant default
  		if (color == null) {
  			return {
  				mode: 'constant',
  				value: [0.13, 0.55, 0.13, 1.0] // Default forest green
  			};
  		}
  
  		// Object form: { field: 'species', scheme: 'category10' }
  		if (typeof color === 'object' && color.field) {
  			return {
  				mode: 'field',
  				field: color.field,
  				scheme: color.scheme || scheme
  			};
  		}
  
  		// String: could be constant ("#ff0000", "red") or field name ("species")
  		if (typeof color === 'string') {
  			if (isColorConstant(color)) {
  				return {
  					mode: 'constant',
  					value: parseColorToRGBA(color)
  				};
  			} else {
  				// Treat as field name
  				return {
  					mode: 'field',
  					field: color,
  					scheme: scheme
  				};
  			}
  		}
  
  		// Fallback
  		return {
  			mode: 'constant',
  			value: [0.13, 0.55, 0.13, 1.0]
  		};
  	});
  
  	// Build select config based on color mode
  	const selectConfig = $derived({
  		x,
  		y,
  		...(colorConfig.mode === 'field' && { color: colorConfig.field })
  	});
  
  	const config = $derived({
  		markType: 'dot',
  		supportedScaleTypes: {
  			x: ['linear', 'identity', 'band', 'point', 'time', 'log'],
  			y: ['linear', 'identity', 'band', 'point', 'time', 'log']
  		},
  		scaleRequirements: {
  			x: { type: preferredXScaleType, priority: SCALE_PRIORITIES.PREFER },
  			y: { type: preferredYScaleType, priority: SCALE_PRIORITIES.PREFER }
  		},
  		table,
  		selection,
  		select: selectConfig,
  		transforms,
  		clientOptions: {
  			limit,
  			filterNulls: filterNulls ? [x, y] : null
  		}
  	});
  
  	let xScaleId = $derived(chartContext.xScaleState?.id);
  	let yScaleId = $derived(chartContext.yScaleState?.id);
  	let instanceCount = $derived(setup?.data?.length || 0);
  
  	// Get or create color scale when in field mode
  	$effect(() => {
  		if (colorConfig.mode === 'field' && scaleRegistry) {
  			untrack(() => {
  				// Request color scale from registry
  				colorScaleState = scaleRegistry.getOrCreateScale(
  					colorConfig.field,
  					'color',
  					{ scheme: colorConfig.scheme },
  					chartContext.chartId
  				);
  			});
  		} else {
  			colorScaleState = null;
  		}
  	});
  
  	// Update color scale domain when data arrives
  	$effect(() => {
  		if (colorConfig.mode === 'field' && colorScaleState && setup?.data) {
  			const colorArray = setup.data.getColumn('color');
  			if (colorArray) {
  				// Extract unique categories for ordinal, or [min, max] for continuous
  				if (colorScaleState.isOrdinal) {
  					const uniqueCategories = [...new Set(colorArray)].filter(v => v != null);
  					colorScaleState.setDomain(uniqueCategories);
  				} else {
  					let min = Infinity, max = -Infinity;
  					for (let i = 0; i < colorArray.length; i++) {
  						const v = colorArray[i];
  						if (v != null && !isNaN(v)) {
  							if (v < min) min = v;
  							if (v > max) max = v;
  						}
  					}
  					if (min !== Infinity) {
  						colorScaleState.setDomain([min, max]);
  					}
  				}
  			}
  		}
  	});
  
  	// X data
  	let xData = $derived.by(() => {
  		if (!setup?.data || setup.data.length === 0) return null;
  
  		const rawArray = setup.data.getColumn('x');
  		if (!rawArray) return null;
  
  		if (setup.actualXScaleType === 'band' || setup.actualXScaleType === 'point') {
  			const processed = chartContext.xScaleState.processDataForGPU(rawArray, setup.markId);
  			return processed instanceof Float32Array ? processed : new Float32Array(processed);
  		}
  
  		return rawArray;
  	});
  
  	// Y data
  	let yData = $derived.by(() => {
  		if (!setup?.data || setup.data.length === 0) return null;
  
  		const rawArray = setup.data.getColumn('y');
  		if (!rawArray) return null;
  
  		if (setup.actualYScaleType === 'band' || setup.actualYScaleType === 'point') {
  			const processed = chartContext.yScaleState.processDataForGPU(rawArray, setup.markId);
  			return processed instanceof Float32Array ? processed : new Float32Array(processed);
  		}
  
  		return rawArray;
  	});
  
  	// Color value data (raw values for GPU lookup, or null for constant mode)
  	let colorValueData = $derived.by(() => {
  		if (colorConfig.mode !== 'field') return null;
  		if (!setup?.data || !instanceCount || !colorScaleState?.ready) return null;
  
  		const colorArray = setup.data.getColumn('color');
  		if (!colorArray) return null;
  
  		// Use ColorScaleState to convert to GPU-ready values
  		return colorScaleState.processDataForGPU(colorArray);
  	});
  
  	// Radius data
  	let radiusData = $derived.by(() => {
  		if (!instanceCount) return null;
  
  		const radii = new Float32Array(instanceCount);
  
  		if (typeof radius === 'function') {
  			const xArray = setup?.data?.getColumn('x');
  			const yArray = setup?.data?.getColumn('y');
  
  			if (!xArray || !yArray) return null;
  
  			for (let i = 0; i < instanceCount; i++) {
  				radii[i] = radius({ x: xArray[i], y: yArray[i] });
  			}
  		} else {
  			radii.fill(radius);
  		}
  
  		return radii;
  	});
  
  	// Alpha data
  	let alphaData = $derived.by(() => {
  		if (!instanceCount) return null;
  
  		const alphas = new Float32Array(instanceCount);
  		alphas.fill(alpha);
  		return alphas;
  	});
  
  	// Props for Dots renderer
  	let colorMode = $derived(colorConfig.mode === 'field' ? 1 : 0);
  	let colorScaleIndex = $derived(colorScaleState?.gpuIndex ?? 0);
  	let constantColor = $derived(
  		colorConfig.mode === 'constant' ? colorConfig.value : [0, 0, 0, 1]
  	);
  
  	// Ready check: include color scale readiness for field mode
  	let colorReady = $derived(
  		colorConfig.mode === 'constant' || (colorScaleState?.ready ?? false)
  	);
  
  	$effect(() => {
  		(void config, xScaleId, yScaleId);
  
  		untrack(() => {
  			setup?.destroy();
  			setup = new BaseMarkSetup(config, coordinator, chartContext, markId);
  		});
  	});
  
  	onDestroy(() => {
  		setup?.destroy();
  	});
  </script>
  
  {#if setup?.gpuReady && colorReady && xData && yData}
  	<Dots
  		{xData}
  		{yData}
  		{colorValueData}
  		{radiusData}
  		{alphaData}
  		{colorMode}
  		{colorScaleIndex}
  		{constantColor}
  		dataVersion={setup.client.dataVersion}
  	/>
  {:else if setup?.client?.error}
  	<text x={chartContext.margin.left} y={chartContext.margin.top} fill="red" font-size="12">
  		Error: {setup.client.error.message}
  	</text>
  {:else if setup?.client?.delayedLoading}
  	<text x={chartContext.margin.left} y={chartContext.margin.top} fill="currentColor" font-size="12">
  		Loading data...
  	</text>
  {:else}
  	<text x={chartContext.margin.left} y={chartContext.margin.top} fill="currentColor" font-size="12">
  		Initializing mark...
  	</text>
  {/if}
</src/lib/components/marks/DotsMark.svelte>

<src/lib/components/marks/BarMarkX.svelte>
  <script>
  	import { getContext, onDestroy, untrack } from 'svelte';
  	import { getScaleState } from '$lib/scales';
  	import { BaseMarkSetup } from './BaseMarkSetup.svelte.js';
  	import { Rects } from '../rendering/index.js';
  	import { SCALE_PRIORITIES } from '$lib/scales/ScalePriorities';
  	import {
  		getSourceType,
  		processData,
  		processColorData,
  		processAlphaData,
  		buildSelectFromChannels
  	} from './utils/index.js';
  
  	let {
  		table,
  		selection,
  		x, // shorthand for x2
  		x1 = 0, // baseline (start of bars) - overridden by stack transform
  		x2 = x, // end of bars (the data values)
  		y, // categorical data
  		color,
  		alpha = 0.8,
  		limit,
  		filterNulls = false,
  		transforms = [], // NEW: Transform pipeline
  
  		padding,
  		paddingInner,
  		paddingOuter,
  		align = 0.5
  	} = $props();
  
  	const markId = `barx-${crypto.randomUUID().slice(0, 8)}`;
  	const coordinator = getContext('mosaic-coordinator');
  	const chartContext = getContext('chart');
  	const colorScale = $derived(getScaleState('color', 'ordinal'));
  
  	let setup = $state(null);
  	let xScaleId = $derived(chartContext.xScaleState?.id);
  	let yScaleId = $derived(chartContext.yScaleState?.id);
  
  	const yScaleConfig = $derived.by(() => {
  		const config = {};
  		if (padding != null) config.padding = padding;
  		if (paddingInner != null) config.paddingInner = paddingInner;
  		if (paddingOuter != null) config.paddingOuter = paddingOuter;
  		if (align != null) config.align = align;
  		return config;
  	});
  
  	const config = $derived({
  		markType: 'barx',
  		supportedScaleTypes: {
  			x: ['linear', 'identity'],
  			y: ['band']
  		},
  		scaleRequirements: {
  			x: { type: 'linear', priority: SCALE_PRIORITIES.PREFER },
  			y: { type: 'band', priority: SCALE_PRIORITIES.MANDATE, config: yScaleConfig }
  		},
  		table,
  		selection,
  		select: buildSelectFromChannels({ x1, x2, y, color }),
  		transforms, // NEW: Pass transforms to setup
  		clientOptions: {
  			limit,
  			filterNulls: filterNulls ? [x1, x2, y] : null
  		}
  	});
  
  	// NEW: Use data accessor
  	let instanceCount = $derived(setup?.data?.length || 0);
  
  	// Check if transforms modified channels (e.g., stack adds x0/x1)
  	let hasStackTransform = $derived(transforms.some((t) => t.type === 'stack'));
  
  	let dataStrategies = $derived({
  		x1: getSourceType(x1),
  		x2: getSourceType(x2),
  		y: getSourceType(y),
  		color: getSourceType(color),
  		alpha: getSourceType(alpha)
  	});
  
  	// X data - use x0/x1 from stack transform if present
  	let x0Data = $derived.by(() => {
  		if (!setup?.data || !instanceCount) return null;
  
  		// If stack transform provided x0, use it
  		if (hasStackTransform) {
  			const x0Array = setup.data.getColumn('x0');
  			if (x0Array) return x0Array;
  		}
  
  		// Otherwise use x1 prop (baseline)
  		if (dataStrategies.x1.type === 'constant') {
  			const arr = new Float32Array(instanceCount);
  			arr.fill(dataStrategies.x1.value);
  			return arr;
  		}
  
  		return setup.data.getColumn('x1');
  	});
  
  	let x1Data = $derived.by(() => {
  		if (!setup?.data || !instanceCount) return null;
  
  		// If stack transform provided x1, use it
  		if (hasStackTransform) {
  			const x1Array = setup.data.getColumn('x1');
  			if (x1Array) return x1Array;
  		}
  
  		// Otherwise use x2 prop (data values)
  		if (dataStrategies.x2.type === 'constant') {
  			const arr = new Float32Array(instanceCount);
  			arr.fill(dataStrategies.x2.value);
  			return arr;
  		}
  
  		return setup.data.getColumn('x2');
  	});
  
  	// Y data - categorical (band scale)
  	let yData = $derived.by(() => {
  		if (!setup?.data || !instanceCount || !setup?.actualYScaleType) return null;
  
  		if (setup.actualYScaleType === 'band') {
  			const rawArray = setup.data.getColumn('y');
  			if (!rawArray) return null;
  
  			const indices = chartContext.yScaleState.processDataForGPU(rawArray, setup.markId);
  
  			// Convert to rectangle coordinates
  			const y0Data = new Float32Array(instanceCount);
  			const y1Data = new Float32Array(instanceCount);
  
  			for (let i = 0; i < instanceCount; i++) {
  				const categoryIndex = indices[i];
  				y0Data[i] = categoryIndex;
  				y1Data[i] = categoryIndex;
  			}
  
  			console.log(`📊 BarMarkX Y data for band scale:`, {
  				instanceCount,
  				rawCategories: Array.from(rawArray.slice(0, 5)),
  				indices: Array.from(indices.slice(0, 5)),
  				y0Data: Array.from(y0Data.slice(0, 5)),
  				y1Data: Array.from(y1Data.slice(0, 5)), // ← Add this
  				scaleReady: chartContext.yScaleState.isReadyForGPU,
  				domain: Array.from(chartContext.yScaleState.effectiveDomain) // ← Convert to array
  			});
  
  			return { y0Data, y1Data };
  		}
  
  		return null;
  	});
  
  	let y0Data = $derived(yData?.y0Data);
  	let y1Data = $derived(yData?.y1Data);
  
  	// Color data
  	let colorData = $derived.by(() => {
  		if (!setup?.data || !instanceCount) return null;
  
  		console.log('🎨 colorData strategy:', dataStrategies.color);
  
  		if (dataStrategies.color === 'constant-color') {
  			// Use the color prop directly!
  			const rgba = new Float32Array(instanceCount * 4);
  			const parsed = parseColor(color); // ← Use 'color' prop, not dataStrategies
  
  			console.log('🎨 Constant color parsed:', parsed);
  
  			for (let i = 0; i < instanceCount; i++) {
  				rgba.set(parsed, i * 4);
  			}
  
  			console.log('🎨 Color array created:', Array.from(rgba.slice(0, 12)));
  
  			return rgba;
  		}
  
  		// Variable color needs colorScale
  		if (!colorScale) return null;
  
  		const colorArray = setup.data.getColumn('color');
  		if (!colorArray) return null;
  
  		const rgba = new Float32Array(instanceCount * 4);
  		for (let i = 0; i < instanceCount; i++) {
  			const colorValue = colorArray[i];
  			const fillColor = colorScale(colorValue);
  			const parsed = parseColor(fillColor);
  			rgba.set(parsed, i * 4);
  		}
  
  		return rgba;
  	});
  
  	// Alpha data
  	let alphaData = $derived.by(() => {
  		if (!instanceCount) return null;
  
  		const alphas = new Float32Array(instanceCount);
  
  		if (dataStrategies.alpha.type === 'constant') {
  			alphas.fill(dataStrategies.alpha.value);
  		} else {
  			const alphaArray = setup.data.getColumn('alpha');
  			if (alphaArray) {
  				for (let i = 0; i < instanceCount; i++) {
  					alphas[i] = alphaArray[i];
  				}
  			} else {
  				alphas.fill(0.8);
  			}
  		}
  
  		return alphas;
  	});
  
  	// Helper to parse color
  	function parseColor(color) {
  		if (typeof color === 'string' && color.startsWith('#')) {
  			const r = parseInt(color.slice(1, 3), 16) / 255;
  			const g = parseInt(color.slice(3, 5), 16) / 255;
  			const b = parseInt(color.slice(5, 7), 16) / 255;
  			return [r, g, b, 1.0];
  		}
  		return [0.5, 0.5, 0.5, 1.0]; // Default gray
  	}
  
  	$effect(() => {
  		(void config, xScaleId, yScaleId);
  
  		untrack(() => {
  			setup?.destroy();
  			setup = new BaseMarkSetup(config, coordinator, chartContext, markId);
  		});
  	});
  
  	onDestroy(() => {
  		setup?.destroy();
  	});
  
    // Temp logging
  	$effect(() => {
  		if (chartContext?.yScaleState?.isReadyForGPU && chartContext.innerHeight) {
  			console.log('📏 Band scale calculations:', {
  				innerHeight: chartContext.innerHeight,
  				categoryCount: chartContext.yScaleState.effectiveDomain.length,
  				bandwidth: chartContext.yScaleState.bandwidth(chartContext.innerHeight),
  				step: chartContext.yScaleState.step(chartContext.innerHeight)
  			});
  		}
  	});
  </script>
  
  {#if setup?.gpuReady && x0Data && x1Data && y0Data && y1Data}
  	<Rects
  		{x0Data}
  		{x1Data}
  		{y0Data}
  		{y1Data}
  		{colorData}
  		{alphaData}
  		dataVersion={setup.client.dataVersion}
  	/>
  {:else if setup?.client?.error}
  	<text x={chartContext.margin.left} y={chartContext.margin.top} fill="red" font-size="12">
  		Error: {setup.client.error.message}
  	</text>
  {:else if setup?.client?.delayedLoading}
  	<text x={chartContext.margin.left} y={chartContext.margin.top} fill="currentColor" font-size="12">
  		Loading data...
  	</text>
  {:else}
  	<text x={chartContext.margin.left} y={chartContext.margin.top} fill="currentColor" font-size="12">
  		Initializing bars...
  	</text>
  {/if}
</src/lib/components/marks/BarMarkX.svelte>

<src/lib/data/DataAccessor.js>
  // src/lib/data/DataAccessor.js
  
  export class ArrowDataAccessor {
    constructor(arrowTable) {
      this.arrowTable = arrowTable;
      this.type = 'arrow';
    }
    
    get length() {
      return this.arrowTable.numRows;
    }
    
    getColumn(name) {
      const col = this.arrowTable.getChild(name);
      if (!col) return null;
      
      const arr = col.toArray();
      if (!arr) return null; // Safety check
      
      // Ensure Float32Array for numeric types
      if (arr instanceof Float32Array) {
        return arr;
      }
      
      if (arr instanceof Float64Array) {
        return new Float32Array(arr);
      }
      
      // Integer types → Float32
      if (arr instanceof Int8Array || arr instanceof Int16Array || 
          arr instanceof Int32Array || arr instanceof Uint8Array || 
          arr instanceof Uint16Array || arr instanceof Uint32Array ||
          arr instanceof BigInt64Array || arr instanceof BigUint64Array) {
        return new Float32Array(arr);
      }
      
      // Non-numeric (strings, dates) - return as-is
      return arr;
    }
    
    toString() {
      return `ArrowDataAccessor(${this.length} rows)`;
    }
  }
  
  export class ObjectDataAccessor {
    constructor(data) {
      this.data = data;
      this.type = 'objects';
      this._columnTypes = new Map();
    }
    
    get length() {
      return this.data.length;
    }
    
    getColumn(name) {
      if (!this.data || this.data.length === 0) return null;
      
      // Infer type from first non-null value
      let columnType = this._columnTypes.get(name);
      
      if (!columnType) {
        const firstValue = this.data.find(d => d[name] != null)?.[name];
        if (firstValue === undefined) {
          // All values are null/undefined
          return new Float32Array(this.data.length); // Return zeros
        }
        columnType = typeof firstValue;
        this._columnTypes.set(name, columnType);
      }
      
      // Numeric columns → Float32Array
      if (columnType === 'number') {
        const arr = new Float32Array(this.data.length);
        for (let i = 0; i < this.data.length; i++) {
          const value = this.data[i]?.[name];
          arr[i] = typeof value === 'number' ? value : 0;
        }
        return arr;
      }
      
      // Non-numeric → plain array
      return this.data.map(d => d?.[name] ?? null);
    }
    
    toString() {
      return `ObjectDataAccessor(${this.length} rows)`;
    }
  }
</src/lib/data/DataAccessor.js>

<src/lib/sql/bin.js>
  // src/lib/sql/bin.js
  
  import { binDate, binHistogram, sql, column } from '@uwdata/mosaic-sql';
  
  /**
   * Bin numeric or temporal data into discrete intervals
   * 
   * Three modes:
   * 1. Explicit numeric: bin('age', { step: 5 })
   * 2. Explicit temporal: bin('date', { interval: 'day' })
   * 3. Auto: bin('age') - uses field statistics
   * 
   * @param {string|Expression} field - Field to bin
   * @param {object} options - Configuration
   * @param {number} options.step - Explicit bin size (numeric)
   * @param {string} options.interval - Explicit interval (temporal: day, week, month, year)
   * @param {number} options.offset - Offset for bin boundaries
   * @param {number} options.thresholds - Target number of bins (auto mode)
   * @param {boolean} options.nice - Use nice round numbers (auto mode)
   * @returns {Expression|AutoBinNode}
   */
  export function bin(field, options = {}) {
    const { step, interval, offset = 0 } = options;
    
    // Explicit numeric binning
    if (step !== undefined) {
      const col = typeof field === 'string' ? column(field) : field;
      return sql`FLOOR((${col} - ${offset}) / ${step}) * ${step} + ${offset}`;
    }
    
    // Explicit temporal binning
    if (interval !== undefined) {
      const col = typeof field === 'string' ? column(field) : field;
      // Use Mosaic's binDate helper
      return binDate(col, null, options);
    }
    
    // Auto mode - defer resolution until we have field statistics
    return new AutoBinNode(field, options);
  }
  
  /**
   * Auto-binning node that defers SQL generation until field statistics are available
   * This allows optimal bin sizing based on actual data range
   */
  export class AutoBinNode {
    constructor(field, options = {}) {
      this.field = field;
      this.options = options;
      this.isAutoBinNode = true; // Marker for detection
    }
    
    /**
     * Get field statistics needed for this bin
     * Called by BaseMarkSetup to build field info requests
     */
    get stats() {
      const fieldName = typeof this.field === 'string' ? this.field : this.field.toString();
      return {
        column: fieldName,
        stats: ['min', 'max']
      };
    }
    
    /**
     * Resolve to concrete SQL expression once field statistics are available
     * @param {object} fieldInfo - Field statistics from DataClient
     * @param {string} scaleType - Scale type (linear, time, etc.)
     * @returns {Expression} Mosaic SQL expression
     */
    resolve(fieldInfo, scaleType) {
      const fieldName = typeof this.field === 'string' ? this.field : this.field.toString();
      const info = fieldInfo[fieldName] || {};
      const { min, max, type } = info;
      
      if (min === undefined || max === undefined) {
        throw new Error(
          `Cannot auto-bin field "${fieldName}": missing min/max statistics. ` +
          `This may happen if the field contains only NULL values or doesn't exist in the table.`
        );
      }
      
      const col = typeof this.field === 'string' ? column(this.field) : this.field;
      
      // Detect if temporal
      const isTemporal = 
        this.options.interval !== undefined ||
        type === 'date' ||
        scaleType === 'time' ||
        scaleType === 'utc';
      
      if (isTemporal) {
        // Use Mosaic's binDate helper
        return binDate(col, [min, max], this.options);
      } else {
        // Use Mosaic's binHistogram helper
        return binHistogram(col, [min, max], this.options);
      }
    }
    
    /**
     * For extent marks (rect, rule), create offset version
     * @param {number} offset - Bin offset
     * @returns {AutoBinNode}
     */
    withOffset(offset) {
      return new AutoBinNode(this.field, {
        ...this.options,
        offset
      });
    }
  }
  
  /**
   * Helper to check if an expression is a bin
   * @param {*} expr
   * @returns {boolean}
   */
  export function isBinExpression(expr) {
    if (!expr) return false;
    
    // Check for AutoBinNode
    if (expr.isAutoBinNode) return true;
    
    // Check for explicit bin SQL (contains FLOOR)
    const sql = expr.toString?.() || '';
    return /FLOOR\s*\(/i.test(sql);
  }
  
  /**
   * Expand bin to extent (x1/x2 or y1/y2) for rect/rule marks
   * @param {Expression|AutoBinNode} binExpr
   * @param {string} channel - 'x' or 'y'
   * @param {string} markType - 'rect', 'rule', etc.
   * @returns {object} Map of channels
   */
  export function expandBinForExtent(binExpr, channel, markType) {
    if (!isBinExpression(binExpr)) {
      return { [channel]: binExpr };
    }
    
    // Check if mark needs extent
    const needsExtent = ['rect', 'rectX', 'rectY', 'rule', 'ruleX', 'ruleY'].includes(markType);
    
    if (!needsExtent) {
      return { [channel]: binExpr };
    }
    
    // Auto bin - create offset version
    if (binExpr.isAutoBinNode) {
      return {
        [`${channel}1`]: binExpr,
        [`${channel}2`]: binExpr.withOffset(1)
      };
    }
    
    // Explicit bin - add offset to SQL
    return {
      [`${channel}1`]: binExpr,
      [`${channel}2`]: sql`${binExpr} + 1`
    };
  }
</src/lib/sql/bin.js>

<src/lib/transforms/TransformCompiler.js>
  // src/lib/transforms/TransformCompiler.js
  
  /**
   * TransformCompiler orchestrates the transform pipeline.
   * 
   * Responsibilities:
   * - Separate SQL and JS transforms
   * - Apply SQL transforms to Query AST
   * - Validate queries remain flat (no CTEs)
   * - Apply JS transforms to Arrow data
   * - Extract additional channels (y0/y1, x0/x1)
   */
  
  export class TransformCompiler {
    constructor(transforms = []) {
      this.transforms = transforms;
      
      // Separate by stage
      this.sqlTransforms = transforms.filter(t => t.stage === 'sql');
      this.jsTransforms = transforms.filter(t => t.stage === 'js');
      
      // Validate ordering (SQL must come before JS)
      this.#validateStaging();
    }
    
    /**
     * Ensure SQL transforms come before JS transforms
     */
    #validateStaging() {
      let seenJS = false;
      
      for (const transform of this.transforms) {
        if (transform.stage === 'js') {
          seenJS = true;
        } else if (transform.stage === 'sql' && seenJS) {
          throw new Error(
            `Invalid transform order: SQL transform "${transform.type}" appears after JS transform. ` +
            `SQL transforms must come before JS transforms in the pipeline.`
          );
        }
      }
    }
    
    /**
     * Apply SQL transforms to query
     * @param {Query} query - Mosaic Query object
     * @returns {Query} Modified query
     */
    applyToQuery(query) {
      let result = query;
      
      for (const transform of this.sqlTransforms) {
        try {
          result = transform.apply(result);
        } catch (error) {
          throw new Error(
            `Failed to apply ${transform.type} transform: ${error.message}`
          );
        }
      }
      
      return result;
    }
    
    /**
     * Apply JS transforms to data
     * @param {Array|ArrowTable} data - Data to transform
     * @param {object} config - Mark configuration (x, y, fill fields)
     * @param {object} scales - Scale objects for context
     * @returns {Array} Transformed data
     */
    applyToData(data, config, scales = {}) {
      let result = Array.isArray(data) ? data : this.#arrowToArray(data);
      
      for (const transform of this.jsTransforms) {
        try {
          result = transform.apply(result, config, scales);
        } catch (error) {
          throw new Error(
            `Failed to apply ${transform.type} transform: ${error.message}`
          );
        }
      }
      
      return result;
    }
    
    /**
     * Convert Arrow table to array of objects
     */
    #arrowToArray(arrowTable) {
      const rows = [];
      const numRows = arrowTable.numRows;
      
      for (let i = 0; i < numRows; i++) {
        const row = {};
        for (const field of arrowTable.schema.fields) {
          const column = arrowTable.getChild(field.name);
          row[field.name] = column.get(i);
        }
        rows.push(row);
      }
      
      return rows;
    }
    
    /**
     * Get additional channels that transforms will add
     * For example, stack() adds y0 and y1
     * @returns {Array<string>} Channel names
     */
    getAdditionalChannels() {
      const channels = [];
      
      for (const transform of this.jsTransforms) {
        if (transform.channels) {
          channels.push(...transform.channels);
        }
      }
      
      return channels;
    }
    
    /**
     * Validate that query is flat (no CTEs, no subqueries)
     * This is critical for Mosaic's PreAggregator optimization
     * @param {Query} query
     * @throws {Error} If query contains CTEs or subqueries
     */
    validateQuery(query) {
      const sql = query.toString();
      
      // Check for WITH clause (CTEs)
      if (/\bWITH\s+/i.test(sql)) {
        throw new Error(
          'Transform pipeline generated a CTE (WITH clause). ' +
          'This breaks Mosaic\'s PreAggregator optimization. ' +
          'CTEs prevent the optimizer from recognizing GROUP BY patterns. ' +
          'Problematic transforms: ' + this.sqlTransforms.map(t => t.type).join(', ')
        );
      }
      
      // Check for subqueries (nested SELECT)
      if (/\(\s*SELECT\s+/i.test(sql)) {
        throw new Error(
          'Transform pipeline generated a subquery. ' +
          'This breaks Mosaic\'s PreAggregator optimization. ' +
          'Subqueries prevent the optimizer from building materialized cubes. ' +
          'Problematic transforms: ' + this.sqlTransforms.map(t => t.type).join(', ')
        );
      }
    }
    
    /**
     * Get human-readable description of the pipeline
     * Useful for debugging and documentation
     * @returns {string}
     */
    describe() {
      const parts = [];
      
      if (this.sqlTransforms.length > 0) {
        parts.push('SQL Pipeline:');
        for (const t of this.sqlTransforms) {
          parts.push(`  - ${t.describe?.() || t.type}`);
        }
      }
      
      if (this.jsTransforms.length > 0) {
        parts.push('JS Pipeline:');
        for (const t of this.jsTransforms) {
          parts.push(`  - ${t.describe?.() || t.type}`);
        }
      }
      
      return parts.join('\n');
    }
    
    /**
     * Check if pipeline has any transforms
     */
    isEmpty() {
      return this.transforms.length === 0;
    }
    
    /**
     * Check if pipeline has SQL transforms
     */
    hasSQLTransforms() {
      return this.sqlTransforms.length > 0;
    }
    
    /**
     * Check if pipeline has JS transforms
     */
    hasJSTransforms() {
      return this.jsTransforms.length > 0;
    }
  }
</src/lib/transforms/TransformCompiler.js>

<src/lib/transforms/index.js>
  // src/lib/transforms/index.js
  
  /**
   * Transform Pipeline for Mosaic-based visualizations
   * 
   * Transforms are divided into two stages:
   * - SQL: Modify the Query AST (WHERE, HAVING, LIMIT, ORDER BY)
   * - JS: Process Arrow table results (topN, stack, dodge, normalize)
   * 
   * SQL transforms must maintain flat queries (no CTEs) to preserve
   * Mosaic's PreAggregator optimization.
   */
  
  import { and, or } from '@uwdata/mosaic-sql';
  import * as d3 from 'd3-array';
  
  // =============================================================================
  // SQL TRANSFORMS (Query Modifiers)
  // =============================================================================
  
  /**
   * Add WHERE clause filter to query
   * @param {Expression} condition - Mosaic SQL expression
   * @example filter(gt('revenue', 0))
   */
  export function filter(condition) {
    return {
      stage: 'sql',
      type: 'filter',
      apply(query) {
        return query.where(condition);
      },
      describe() {
        return `Filter: ${condition.toString()}`;
      }
    };
  }
  
  /**
   * Add HAVING clause (post-aggregation filter)
   * @param {Expression} condition - Mosaic SQL expression
   * @example having(gt(sum('revenue'), 10000))
   */
  export function having(condition) {
    return {
      stage: 'sql',
      type: 'having',
      apply(query) {
        return query.having(condition);
      },
      describe() {
        return `Having: ${condition.toString()}`;
      }
    };
  }
  
  /**
   * Limit number of rows returned
   * @param {number} n - Maximum rows
   * @example limit(100)
   */
  export function limit(n) {
    return {
      stage: 'sql',
      type: 'limit',
      apply(query) {
        return query.limit(n);
      },
      describe() {
        return `Limit: ${n}`;
      }
    };
  }
  
  /**
   * Order results by expression(s)
   * @param {...Expression} expressions - Mosaic SQL expressions
   * @example orderby(desc('revenue'))
   */
  export function orderby(...expressions) {
    return {
      stage: 'sql',
      type: 'orderby',
      apply(query) {
        return query.orderby(...expressions);
      },
      describe() {
        return `Order by: ${expressions.map(e => e.toString()).join(', ')}`;
      }
    };
  }
  
  // =============================================================================
  // JS TRANSFORMS (Data Processors)
  // =============================================================================
  
  /**
   * Take top N items by a field, optionally grouping rest as "Other"
   * @param {number} n - Number of top items to keep
   * @param {object} options - Configuration
   * @param {string} options.by - Field to sort by (defaults to y)
   * @param {boolean} options.includeOthers - Group remaining as "Other"
   * @example topN(10, { by: 'revenue', includeOthers: true })
   */
  export function topN(n, options = {}) {
    return {
      stage: 'js',
      type: 'topN',
      apply(data, config) {
        if (!data || data.length === 0) return data;
        
        const by = options.by || config.y;
        
        // Sort descending
        const sorted = [...data].sort((a, b) => (b[by] || 0) - (a[by] || 0));
        
        if (sorted.length <= n) {
          return sorted;
        }
        
        const top = sorted.slice(0, n);
        
        if (options.includeOthers) {
          const others = sorted.slice(n);
          const otherRow = { [config.x]: 'Other' };
          
          // Sum all numeric fields
          for (const key of Object.keys(others[0])) {
            if (typeof others[0][key] === 'number') {
              otherRow[key] = others.reduce((sum, d) => sum + (d[key] || 0), 0);
            }
          }
          
          return [...top, otherRow];
        }
        
        return top;
      },
      describe() {
        return `Top ${n}${options.includeOthers ? ' (with Others)' : ''}`;
      }
    };
  }
  
  // src/lib/transforms/index.js
  
  export function stack(options = {}) {
    const { direction = 'vertical' } = options; // 'vertical' or 'horizontal'
    
    return {
      stage: 'js',
      type: 'stack',
      apply(data, config) {
        if (!data || data.length === 0) return data;
        
        // Determine grouping and value fields based on direction
        const groupField = direction === 'vertical' ? config.x : config.y;
        const valueField = direction === 'vertical' ? config.y : config.x;
        
        // Group by category
        const groups = d3.group(data, d => d[groupField]);
        
        const stacked = [];
        for (const [category, items] of groups) {
          let offset = 0;
          for (const item of items) {
            const value = item[valueField] || 0;
            
            if (direction === 'vertical') {
              stacked.push({
                ...item,
                y0: offset,
                y1: offset + value
              });
            } else {
              stacked.push({
                ...item,
                x0: offset,
                x1: offset + value
              });
            }
            
            offset += value;
          }
        }
        
        return stacked;
      },
      channels: direction === 'vertical' ? ['y0', 'y1'] : ['x0', 'x1']
    };
  }
  
  /**
   * Normalize values to percentages within each x group
   * @param {object} options - Configuration
   * @example normalize()
   */
  export function normalize(options = {}) {
    return {
      stage: 'js',
      type: 'normalize',
      apply(data, config) {
        if (!data || data.length === 0) return data;
        
        const xField = config.x;
        const yField = config.y;
        
        // Group by x dimension
        const groups = d3.group(data, d => d[xField]);
        
        const normalized = [];
        for (const [x, items] of groups) {
          const total = d3.sum(items, d => d[yField] || 0);
          
          for (const item of items) {
            const value = item[yField] || 0;
            normalized.push({
              ...item,
              [yField]: total > 0 ? (value / total) * 100 : 0,
              // Also normalize y0/y1 if they exist (from stacking)
              ...(item.y0 !== undefined && { y0: total > 0 ? (item.y0 / total) * 100 : 0 }),
              ...(item.y1 !== undefined && { y1: total > 0 ? (item.y1 / total) * 100 : 0 })
            });
          }
        }
        
        return normalized;
      },
      describe() {
        return 'Normalize to percentages';
      }
    };
  }
  
  /**
   * Dodge (offset) items horizontally for grouped bar charts
   * @param {object} options - Configuration
   * @param {number} options.bandwidth - Override bandwidth calculation
   * @example dodge()
   */
  export function dodge(options = {}) {
    return {
      stage: 'js',
      type: 'dodge',
      apply(data, config, scales) {
        if (!data || data.length === 0) return data;
        
        const xField = config.x;
        
        // Get bandwidth from scale or options
        const bandwidth = scales?.x?.bandwidth?.() || options.bandwidth || 20;
        
        // Group by x dimension
        const groups = d3.group(data, d => d[xField]);
        
        const dodged = [];
        for (const [x, items] of groups) {
          const offset = bandwidth / items.length;
          
          items.forEach((item, i) => {
            dodged.push({
              ...item,
              x0: item[xField] + (i * offset),
              x1: item[xField] + ((i + 1) * offset)
            });
          });
        }
        
        return dodged;
      },
      channels: ['x0', 'x1'],
      describe() {
        return 'Dodge by group';
      }
    };
  }
  
  export { TransformCompiler } from './TransformCompiler'
</src/lib/transforms/index.js>

<src/lib/transforms/integration.test.js>
  // src/lib/transforms/integration.test.js
  
  import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
  import { Query, sum, count, gt, column } from '@uwdata/mosaic-sql';
  import { filter, topN, stack } from './index.js';
  import { TransformCompiler } from './TransformCompiler.js';
  import { bin } from '../sql/bin.js';
  
  describe('Transform Pipeline Integration', () => {
    it('full pipeline: SQL + JS transforms', () => {
      // Simulate a bar chart with stacking
      const transforms = [
        filter(gt('revenue', 0)),
        topN(10, { includeOthers: true }),
        stack()
      ];
      
      const compiler = new TransformCompiler(transforms);
      
      // Build query
      let query = Query.from('sales')
        .select({
          x: 'product',
          y: sum('revenue'),
          fill: 'region'
        })
        .groupby('product', 'region');
      
      // Apply SQL transforms
      query = compiler.applyToQuery(query);
      
      // Validate flat query
      expect(() => compiler.validateQuery(query)).not.toThrow();
      
      const sql = query.toString();
      expect(sql).toContain('WHERE revenue > 0');
      expect(sql).not.toContain('WITH'); // No CTE
      
      // Simulate query results
      const mockData = [
        { x: 'A', y: 1000, fill: 'North' },
        { x: 'A', y: 500, fill: 'South' },
        { x: 'B', y: 800, fill: 'North' },
        // ... more data
      ];
      
      // Apply JS transforms
      const result = compiler.applyToData(mockData, {
        x: 'x',
        y: 'y',
        fill: 'fill'
      });
      
      // Verify stacking
      expect(result[0]).toHaveProperty('y0');
      expect(result[0]).toHaveProperty('y1');
    });
    
    it('auto binning with transforms', () => {
      const x = bin('age'); // Auto bin
      const y = sum('revenue');
      
      // Check that AutoBinNode needs stats
      expect(x.isAutoBinNode).toBe(true);
      expect(x.stats).toEqual({
        column: 'age',
        stats: ['min', 'max']
      });
      
      // Simulate getting field stats
      const fieldInfo = {
        age: { min: 18, max: 75, type: 'number' }
      };
      
      // Resolve bin
      const resolvedX = x.resolve(fieldInfo, 'linear');
      
      // Build query with resolved bin
      const query = Query.from('sales')
        .select({
          x: resolvedX,
          y: y
        })
        .groupby(resolvedX);
      
      const sql = query.toString();
      expect(sql).toContain('FLOOR');
      expect(sql).toContain('GROUP BY');
    });
  });
</src/lib/transforms/integration.test.js>

<src/lib/transforms/transforms.test.js>
  // src/lib/transforms/transforms.test.js
  
  import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
  import { filter, topN, stack, normalize } from './index.js';
  import { TransformCompiler } from './TransformCompiler.js';
  import { bin, AutoBinNode } from '../sql/bin.js';
  import { Query, gt, sum, column } from '@uwdata/mosaic-sql';
  
  describe('Transforms', () => {
    describe('SQL Transforms', () => {
      it('filter() adds WHERE clause', () => {
        const q = Query.from('sales').select({ x: 'product', y: sum('revenue') });
        
        const transform = filter(gt('revenue', 0));
        const result = transform.apply(q);
        
        const sql = result.toString();
        expect(sql).toContain('WHERE');
        expect(sql).toContain('revenue > 0');
      });
    });
    
    describe('JS Transforms', () => {
      it('topN() takes top items', () => {
        const data = [
          { x: 'A', y: 100 },
          { x: 'B', y: 200 },
          { x: 'C', y: 50 }
        ];
        
        const transform = topN(2);
        const result = transform.apply(data, { x: 'x', y: 'y' });
        
        expect(result).toHaveLength(2);
        expect(result[0].x).toBe('B'); // Highest
        expect(result[1].x).toBe('A');
      });
      
      it('topN() includes Others', () => {
        const data = [
          { x: 'A', y: 100 },
          { x: 'B', y: 200 },
          { x: 'C', y: 50 }
        ];
        
        const transform = topN(2, { includeOthers: true });
        const result = transform.apply(data, { x: 'x', y: 'y' });
        
        expect(result).toHaveLength(3);
        expect(result[2].x).toBe('Other');
        expect(result[2].y).toBe(50); // Sum of C
      });
      
      it('stack() calculates y0 and y1', () => {
        const data = [
          { x: 'Jan', y: 10, region: 'North' },
          { x: 'Jan', y: 20, region: 'South' },
          { x: 'Feb', y: 15, region: 'North' }
        ];
        
        const transform = stack();
        const result = transform.apply(data, { x: 'x', y: 'y' });
        
        expect(result).toHaveLength(3);
        expect(result[0]).toHaveProperty('y0', 0);
        expect(result[0]).toHaveProperty('y1', 10);
        expect(result[1]).toHaveProperty('y0', 10);
        expect(result[1]).toHaveProperty('y1', 30);
      });
    });
    
    describe('TransformCompiler', () => {
      it('separates SQL and JS transforms', () => {
        const transforms = [
          filter(gt('revenue', 0)),
          topN(10),
          stack()
        ];
        
        const compiler = new TransformCompiler(transforms);
        
        expect(compiler.sqlTransforms).toHaveLength(1);
        expect(compiler.jsTransforms).toHaveLength(2);
      });
      
      it('throws on invalid ordering', () => {
        const transforms = [
          topN(10),        // JS
          filter(gt('x', 0)) // SQL - wrong order!
        ];
        
        expect(() => new TransformCompiler(transforms)).toThrow(/Invalid transform order/);
      });
      
      it('applies SQL transforms to query', () => {
        const compiler = new TransformCompiler([
          filter(gt('revenue', 0))
        ]);
        
        const q = Query.from('sales').select({ y: sum('revenue') });
        const result = compiler.applyToQuery(q);
        
        expect(result.toString()).toContain('WHERE revenue > 0');
      });
      
      it('detects CTEs', () => {
        const compiler = new TransformCompiler([]);
        
        // Simulate a query with CTE
        const badQuery = {
          toString: () => 'WITH temp AS (SELECT * FROM t) SELECT * FROM temp'
        };
        
        expect(() => compiler.validateQuery(badQuery)).toThrow(/CTE/);
      });
    });
    
    describe('Auto Binning', () => {
      it('creates AutoBinNode by default', () => {
        const result = bin('age');
        
        expect(result).toBeInstanceOf(AutoBinNode);
        expect(result.isAutoBinNode).toBe(true);
      });
      
      it('creates explicit SQL for step', () => {
        const result = bin('age', { step: 5 });
        
        expect(result.isAutoBinNode).toBeFalsy();
        expect(result.toString()).toContain('FLOOR');
      });
      
      it('resolves AutoBinNode with field stats', () => {
        const autoBin = bin('age');
        
        const fieldInfo = {
          age: { min: 18, max: 75, type: 'number' }
        };
        
        const resolved = autoBin.resolve(fieldInfo, 'linear');
        
        // Should return a Mosaic SQL expression
        expect(resolved.toString()).toContain('FLOOR');
      });
    });
  });
</src/lib/transforms/transforms.test.js>

<src/routes/scatter_plot_matrix/+page.svelte>
  <script>
  	import { Coordinator, coordinator, wasmConnector } from '@uwdata/mosaic-core';
  	import { loadCSV, loadParquet } from '@uwdata/mosaic-sql';
  	// import { setScaleRegistry } from "$lib/scales";
  	import { setCoordinators } from '$lib';
  	import { onMount, onDestroy, setContext } from 'svelte';
  	import MainView from './MainView.svelte';
  
  	// import { startProMotionFix, stopProMotionFix } from '$lib/utils/proMotionFix.js';
  	import { startProMotionFix } from '$lib/utils/proMotionFix.js';
  
  	// setScaleRegistry();
  	setCoordinators();
  
  	let coord;
  	// setContext('mosaic-coordinator', coord);
  
  	let isLoaded = $state(false);
  	let loadError = $state(null);
  
  	const logs = [];
  	const queryLogger = {
  		...console,
  
  		debug(...values) {
  			if (values.length > 0 && typeof values[0] === 'string' && values[0].includes('Query')) {
  				const queryLog = values[1];
  
  				if (queryLog && queryLog.sql) {
  					const timestamp = new Date().toLocaleTimeString('sv-SE', { hour12: false });
  					console.log(`[${timestamp}] \n${queryLog.sql}`);
  					logs.push({ query: queryLog.sql, time: new Date() });
  				}
  			}
  		},
  		getLogs() {
  			return logs;
  		}
  	};
  
  	let frameTimes = [];
  	let lastVsync = 0;
  
  	function measureRAF(time) {
  		if (lastVsync > 0) {
  			const delta = time - lastVsync;
  			frameTimes.push(delta);
  
  			// Detect stutters in real-time
  			if (delta > 12) {
  				console.warn(`RAF gap: ${delta.toFixed(1)}ms at ${time.toFixed(0)}`);
  			}
  		}
  		lastVsync = time;
  
  		if (frameTimes.length === 500) {
  			// Analyze distribution
  			const sorted = [...frameTimes].sort((a, b) => a - b);
  			console.log('RAF timing analysis:');
  			console.log(`  Min: ${sorted[0].toFixed(2)}ms`);
  			console.log(`  Max: ${sorted[sorted.length - 1].toFixed(2)}ms`);
  			console.log(`  Median: ${sorted[250].toFixed(2)}ms`);
  			console.log(`  p95: ${sorted[475].toFixed(2)}ms`);
  			console.log(`  p99: ${sorted[495].toFixed(2)}ms`);
  			console.log(`  Stutters (>12ms): ${frameTimes.filter((t) => t > 12).length}`);
  
  			// Show histogram
  			const buckets = {};
  			frameTimes.forEach((t) => {
  				const bucket = Math.floor(t / 2) * 2;
  				buckets[bucket] = (buckets[bucket] || 0) + 1;
  			});
  			console.log('  Distribution:', buckets);
  
  			frameTimes = [];
  		}
  
  		requestAnimationFrame(measureRAF);
  	}
  
  	onMount(async () => {
  		// startProMotionFix();
      // requestAnimationFrame(measureRAF);
  
  		try {
  			const wasm = wasmConnector();
  			coord = new Coordinator();
  			coord.databaseConnector(wasm);
  
  			// coord.manager.logQueries(true);
  			// coord.manager.logger(queryLogger);
  
  			setContext('mosaic-coordinator', coord);
  
  			await coord.exec(loadParquet('penguins', `${window.location.origin}/data/penguins.parquet`));
  
  			isLoaded = true;
  		} catch (error) {
  			console.error('Initialization error:', error);
  			loadError = error.message;
  		}
  	});
  </script>
  
  <div class="app-container">
  	{#if loadError}
  		<div class="error-container">Error loading data: {loadError}</div>
  	{:else if !isLoaded}
  		<div class="loading-container">Loading parquet data...</div>
  	{:else}
  		<MainView />
  	{/if}
  </div>
  
  <style>
  	.app-container {
  		width: 100%;
  		min-height: 100vh;
  		display: flex;
  		flex-direction: column;
  	}
  
  	.loading-container,
  	.error-container {
  		display: flex;
  		justify-content: center;
  		align-items: center;
  		min-height: 200px;
  		padding: 2rem;
  	}
  </style>
</src/routes/scatter_plot_matrix/+page.svelte>
